| iter-v3/079 | 2026-05-16 | NEW RISK PRIMITIVE: a conviction-weighted per-trade position-sizing primitive (primitive 13) — a `conviction_derate(confidence)` map added to `lgbm.py` and wired into `get_signal`, replacing the hardcoded `weight = 100`. The map consumes the M1 model's per-trade directional `confidence` (previously computed inside `get_signal` then discarded) and de-rates low-conviction trades' position weight. Three a-priori constants: `C_FLOOR = 0.50`, `C_REF = 0.65`, `W_MIN_FRAC = 0.50`; returns an int weight in `[50, 100]`; `round(100 × clip((confidence − C_FLOOR)/(C_REF − C_FLOOR), W_MIN_FRAC, 1.0))`. Pure post-model arithmetic transform — no feature added, no labeling change, no selection-gate change, no Optuna change. Secondary edit: REVERT /078's closed universe-revision axis — `V3_MODELS` BCH/ADA/TRX → BCH/LDO/TRX (baseline-restore, not a second axis). NULL-RESULT is the canonical match on THREE independent grounds: (i) Section 8.5 precedes 8.3 INERT in the disjunctive order, (ii) the `<15%` behavioral-saturation condition is met exactly, (iii) INERT's own clause requires `≥15%` de-rated so INERT cannot fire at 7.5%. SUSPICIOUS does NOT fire (IS shift +0.0052 ≥ 0 fails the OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode `IS shift < 0` clause; ratio 0.3368 < 3.0). ROOT CAUSE of the 3× behavioral-effect miss (predicted ≥25%, observed 7.5%): the `/067 _inference_threshold_floor = 0.60` plus Optuna's per-`(symbol,month)` confidence thresholds concentrating at/above `C_REF = 0.65` collapsed the effective de-rate window `[0.60, 0.65)` toward empty. C_REF=0.65 was placed at the threshold-saturation point of the confidence distribution, not its busy region. UNDER-TESTED, NOT DEAD (Critic Adjudication #3): the directional hypothesis has WEAK BUT REAL support — the 12 de-rated (low-conviction) IS trades DID underperform (mean net_pnl_pct -0.019% vs +0.476% non-de-rated; WR 33.3% vs 38.0%); a genuinely dead axis would show de-rated trades performing at/above the non-de-rated group. PURE-WEIGHT-SCALAR + LOOK-AHEAD-CLEAN — both verified: the Critic ran the full 5-step look-ahead chain audit (the conviction-derate reads the M1 model's past-only inference confidence; pure arithmetic transform) and confirmed the pure-weight-scalar property is STRUCTURALLY GUARANTEED (the map never returns 0 → no trade suppressed → IS+OOS roster bit-identical to /077's /060-config roster, 0 added/0 removed, IS duration delta 0.0000); the OOS +0.0713 is largely a data-extent artifact (the last LDO `end_of_data` trade extended ~2 candles between the /077 and /079 runs). PBO 0.1278 + `frac_positive_paths` 0.6444 bit-identical to the anchor — a clean 7.5%-incidence-weight-scalar null signature. **/079 is the 1st cycle-2 NULL-RESULT; the risk-primitive axis is now 2 INERT (/074, /075) + 1 NULL-RESULT (/079) in cycle 2 — the most-tried-least-productive cycle-2 category.** See Caveats — iter-v3/079 below. |

So the OOS +1.1897 is **NOT a look-ahead leak** — it is the iter-v3/026/027/030/034/036/037/102 IS-collapse / OOS-spike overfitting signature (an OOS spike sitting on a collapsed IS fit); /105 is the direct structural twin of /102. The brief's Section 4/7 explicit sub-prediction that "F2 IS-collapse is specifically UNLIKELY because /105 adds NO feature and does not widen the Optuna search space" was **WRONG** — a category error (ruling out one *mechanism* — a 15th feature widening the search space — and concluding the *outcome* was structurally absent); a *different* mechanism (the trend-scanning label is a different and noisier estimand the multi-seed Optuna fit overfits in-sample) produced the same IS-collapse. **THE KEY META-FINDING (Critic Rec 3): iter-v3/105 FALSIFIES the /104 hypothesis that "the label geometry / the estimand is the binding constraint."** The trend-scanning label was the literal test of that hypothesis; the IS-only EDA confirmed the re-framing is real and substantive (+31-52% feature→label IC, robustness-checked against the longest-wins confound) — and if "the label was the constraint" were true, a label the features predict 31-52% better should have *lifted* the IS fit. It did the opposite: the IS fit **collapsed −0.61**. Re-framing the estimand to trend-scanning did NOT lift the IS fit; it collapsed it. The binding constraint is **DOWNSTREAM of the training label** — in the **trade-construction / exit / risk layer**: the mechanism is a *mismatch* — the model was trained on a trend-scanning label (per-bar data-selected-horizon trend sign) but trades were executed through the unchanged triple-barrier execution geometry (fixed ±ATR barrier, fixed 21-candle timeout). A better-predicted training label *geometrically mismatched to the execution layer* does not translate into trade-Sharpe. The generalizable lesson: **a better feature→label IC does NOT predict trade-Sharpe when the training label and the triple-barrier execution geometry are mismatched** — feature→label IC is a property of the labeling problem, not a sufficient statistic for the trade-construction problem when label geometry ≠ execution geometry. ## Caveats — iter-v3/009 INFORMATIONAL OOS flags

The +1.1223 OOS Sharpe in iter-v3/009 must NOT be read as evidence of edge by any future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR. The following caveats are catalogued for audit-trail discipline (`feedback_trade_rate_floor` operative across iterations):

- **OOS_concentration = 98.61%** (LDO-driven). LDOUSDT contributed +70.77% weighted_pnl out of +53.22% total OOS PnL — the other three symbols sum to -17.5% across 75 trades.
- **LDO 12 trades, 75% WR, exact-binomial 95% CI [42.8%, 94.5%]**. CI width is too wide to claim signal; the 75% WR is consistent with a lucky 12-flip sequence on a fair coin within 95% confidence.
- **MKR_OOS regression**: iter-v3/007's MKR weighted_pnl was -6.5% on the matching window-equivalent → iter-v3/009's MKR is -13.1% on a comparable trade count, arguing against any "redundancy drop helps" reading at the per-symbol level.
- **OOS_trades = 87 < 130 trade-rate floor** (`feedback_trade_rate_floor`). Sharpe from <130 trades is structurally underpowered (`σ_SR ≈ √(1/T)` makes the t-test unreliable below this floor).
- **DSR = 0.0, PSR = 1.0**: single-seed exploration artifact (DSR collapses, PSR saturates at N=1). NOT evidence of edge significance.

The +1.12 OOS Sharpe is the highest in v3 track history, but the four caveats above mean it is NOT eligible for downstream bundling without independent multi-seed validation that explicitly pre-registers an OOS-axis falsifier.

## Caveats — iter-v3/010 EXPLORATION-PROMISING flags

The +1.8122 OOS Sharpe and +0.5683 IS Sharpe in iter-v3/010 are the highest in v3 track history. Unlike iter-v3/009, iter-v3/010's headline metrics co-direct (IS lift broad-based across 3 of 4 symbols) and ALL pre-registered falsifiers were NOT triggered, so the verdict is genuinely PROMISING. The following caveats are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **Mechanism cited**: (c) tighter labels reduce label noise so features find real momentum more cleanly. NOT (a) "edge was always there but masked" (would predict neutral/worse PBO; actual PBO improved 0.1145 → 0.1077). NOT (b) regime-specific tuning (lift is broad-based, not symbol-specific).
- **MKR_OOS = -10.6%**: 3rd consecutive negative across iter-v3/007 (MKR -14.03%), iter-v3/009 (MKR -13.1%/-17.61%), iter-v3/010 (MKR -10.65%). Per QR Clarification 2 disposition: revisit at 6-7 consecutive negatives with a per-symbol-diagnostic EXPLORATION (drop MKR as a single-axis universe variation); until then, audit-trail-only.
- **n_high_pbo_cells = 5/175**: TRX/2025-10, TRX/2025-11, MKR/2024-10, MKR/2025-04, MKR/2025-07. Mean PBO aggregator dilutes these to a clean 0.1077; future CONFIRMATION QRs may use `(1 - max_per_cell_pbo)` for a more conservative weighting alongside or instead of `(1 - mean_pbo)`.
- **OOS_trade_rate = 8.07/month < 10/month floor** (`feedback_trade_rate_floor`): informational at EXPLORATION; **floor applies at the BUNDLE level at CONFIRMATION** per the new memory rule `feedback_trade_rate_floor_bundle_level` (added per Critic Rec #3, pre-committed in this catalog row before any CONFIRMATION outcome is known to prevent post-hoc rationalization).
- **OOS_concentration = 57.17%** (BCH-driven): above the 35% per-symbol cap that would apply at CONFIRMATION. Informational under EXPLORATION per brief Section 6.3 — the labeling change may shift concentration in either direction at higher seed counts.
- **Calibration miss**: IS Sharpe overshoots predicted [+0.10, +0.30] band (P4 at 50%) by +0.27 in the FAVORABLE direction. Combined with iter-v3/009's unfavorable undershoot (P4 at 60%, [+0.18, +0.28]; reality +0.0802), labeling-axis EXPLORATION priors should widen the PROMISING band to roughly [+0.05, +0.60] to capture the higher-than-expected variance under colsample=1.0 + n_trials=10.

The five caveats above are NOT disqualifying for catalog inclusion — they are explicit pre-commitments that travel with the catalog row to the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR. iter-v3/010 IS a strong candidate for next CONFIRMATION bundling.

## Caveats — iter-v3/011 EXPLORATION-PROMISING (with lottery/concentration caveats) flags

The +1.6251 OOS Sharpe and +0.9566 IS Sharpe in iter-v3/011 are the second-highest OOS and **highest IS** in v3 track history. The IS axis is broad-based 286 trades (NOT lottery-driven) but the OOS axis carries an LDO 86.31% lottery concentration. Verdict is PROMISING-with-caveats because the falsifier-grade IS axis cleared with substantial margin (+0.56 above the +0.40 PROMISING threshold) on a broad-based sample, while the OOS lottery-flag is informational only under EXPLORATION. The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **Gate-orthogonal MKR** (4th consecutive OOS-negative; threshold compressed 6-7 → 5; next negative = mandatory per-symbol-diagnostic axis): MKR -25.75% OOS net PnL at 25.0% WR — both worst-yet in the trajectory (-6.5% → -13.1% → -10.6% → -25.75%; WR 33.3% → 30.8% → 29.4% → 25.0%). Tighter z-gate INCREASED MKR kill-rate by ~15pp (35.0% → 49.8% — the LARGEST of any symbol) yet WORSENED per-trade economics. **MKR pattern is gate-orthogonal**: tightening doesn't fix it. Per Critic FINAL Recommendation 3, the per-symbol-exclusion threshold is compressed from 6-7 (per iter-v3/010) → 5 consecutive negatives. If iter-v3/012+ records MKR's 5th consecutive negative, the NEXT EXPLORATION must be a per-symbol-diagnostic axis (e.g., drop-MKR universe-change exploration as single axis). Pre-committed via new memory rule `feedback_mkr_threshold_compression.md` to prevent post-hoc rationalization. Rule cannot be renegotiated post-hoc by future Engineer or QR.
- **LDO 86.31% OOS concentration (lottery-flag)**: 10 trades, 8 wins (80% WR), exact-binomial 95% CI **[44.4%, 97.5%]** — CI width too wide to claim signal-from-noise distinction; the 80% WR is consistent with a lucky 10-flip sequence within 95% confidence. Without LDO's 8 wins, OOS Sharpe collapses materially. Future CONFIRMATION QR must scope ex-LDO basket fragility before bundling iter-v3/011 risk-gate component.
- **IS broad-based confirmation** (286 trades, 36.4% WR, +0.96 Sharpe — NOT lottery-driven): IS axis attribution is BCH 100 trades / +86.82% PnL + LDO 21 / +52.90% + TRX 88 / -19.96% + MKR 77 / -23.21%. 121 of 286 IS trades (42%) on the two positive-PnL symbols. The IS-axis broad-based-ness is what justifies the PROMISING verdict alongside the OOS lottery-flag — it forecloses any "the entire iteration is lottery-driven" reading.
- **Floor-recoverable bundle math**: iter-v3/011 single-seed = 101 OOS trades; predicted bundle at CONFIRMATION = 5 outer seeds × 3-4× ensemble = **303-404 OOS bundle trades** (well above the 130-trade floor with margin). Factor 3-4× **inherited from iter-v3/010 review, UNVERIFIED at iter-v3/011's tighter gate setting** — the gate may shift the multiplication factor. A future CONFIRMATION QR must validate the multiplier empirically before claiming the floor clears at bundle level.
- **Data-regen audit note**: First launch failed staleness check (19.6h > 16h threshold per `feedback_data_staleness_per_worktree`); re-fetched 2 klines × 5 symbols + regenerated features (~3 min of the 8 min wall-clock). 0.5% data-extent drift on a 13.5-month OOS window is **NOT attributed to the −0.187 OOS Sharpe metric delta vs iter-v3/010** — gate-axis kill-rate shifts (+15-18pp across all 4 symbols) are the dominant driver. Per-spec staleness guard functioning correctly. Audit-noted but not a methodology issue.

The five caveats above are NOT disqualifying for catalog inclusion. iter-v3/011 IS a strong candidate for next CONFIRMATION bundling, conditional on the future bundling QR addressing the LDO lottery-flag and ex-LDO basket fragility explicitly.

## Caveats — iter-v3/012 EXPLORATION-NEGATIVE-no-effect (NULL-RESULT) flags

The IS Sharpe -0.147 in iter-v3/012 IS a weighted-PnL accounting artifact, NOT a signal-quality change. The trade roster is byte-identical to iter-v3/011 (286 IS, 101 OOS — load-bearing for NULL-RESULT classification). Cataloguing as PROMISING (because IS Sharpe +0.81 > +0.40 threshold) would mislead future CONFIRMATION QR into bundling ±15% as ingredient when finding is "band width in (15%, 20%) is structurally inert over v3 data extent." The five iter-v3/012-specific caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`verdict = NEGATIVE-no-effect`** (NULL-RESULT subtype, hypothesis unsupported): IS Sharpe direction is wrong (-0.147), trade roster IDENTICAL to iter-v3/011 (286 IS, 101 OOS — bit-identical including LDO/MKR per-symbol metrics; PBO 0.1077 bit-identical; n_eff 7 bit-identical). The 17 additional BTC-filter kills (26 → 43; +4.39pp fire rate) materialized as zero-weight rows reducing IS total_pnl 97.31% → 73.42% but did NOT displace any trades. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate; adds zero information to the bundle relative to iter-v3/011. Distinct from iter-v3/009's `NEGATIVE-failed-axis` (which had IS Sharpe below Falsifier 1 threshold AND a failed IS axis).

- **`mkr_rule_triggered = TRUE`** (5/5 consecutive negative trajectory; threshold compressed 6-7 → 5 in iter-v3/011 review FIRED): MKR OOS PnL trajectory −6.5% → −13.1% → −10.6% → −25.75% → **−25.75%** (iter-v3/012 STATIONARY identity with iter-v3/011 strengthens diagnostic case). MKR OOS WR trajectory 33.3% → 30.8% → 29.4% → 25.0% → **25.0%** (worst stationary). Tighter BTC band had ZERO behavioral effect on MKR's 16 OOS trades. **MKR pattern is gate-orthogonal AND BTC-band-orthogonal**: tightening neither z-gate nor BTC-band fixes it. Per `feedback_mkr_threshold_compression.md` (SHA `b9ebbb2`), iter-v3/013 MUST be drop-MKR per-symbol-diagnostic single-axis EXPLORATION (3-symbol BCH+LDO+TRX universe). Cannot be post-hoc renegotiated by future Engineer/QR.

- **`pm25_deferred = TRUE, earliest = iter-v3/014`**: looser-direction ±25% BTC band test deferred per MKR rule override. The MKR rule overrides any other axis preference at iter-v3/013; ±25% test cannot launch until at minimum iter-v3/014 (post-MKR-diagnostic), and only if no further mandatory rule overrides fire. Falsifier 1 NOT triggered would have prevented activation regardless.

- **`n_high_pbo_cells_99 = 4`** (TRX/2025-10 = 1.00, TRX/2025-11 = 1.00, MKR/2025-04 = 0.995, MKR/2025-07 = 0.991): 2 NEW TRX/2025-Q4 entries with PBO = 1.00 in OOS-extending months — particularly concerning because they suggest either regime drift or per-cell sample-size collapse at the OOS frontier. Mean aggregator dilutes these to a clean 0.1077 PBO (bit-identical to iter-v3/011) but the right-tail thickening is real. **Future CONFIRMATION QR uses `(1 − max_per_cell_pbo)` not just `(1 − mean_pbo)` for aggregation discipline** — carried forward from iter-v3/010 + iter-v3/011 audit trail and now reinforced.

- **`trade_roster_identity = TRUE`** (286 IS, 101 OOS bit-identical to iter-v3/011 — **LOAD-BEARING for null-result classification**): without trade-roster bit-identity, IS Sharpe +0.81 alone would qualify as PROMISING (>+0.40 threshold). With it, the "behavioral effect of axis perturbation = 0" reading dominates the Sharpe-number reading. The Critic FINAL preserves this distinction explicitly: *"Cataloguing PROMISING would mislead future CONFIRMATION QR into bundling ±15% as ingredient when finding is 'band width in (15%, 20%) is structurally inert.'"* Future Critic FINAL writeups should preserve the NEGATIVE-no-effect (NULL-RESULT) vs NEGATIVE-failed-axis distinction explicitly.

The five caveats above are LOAD-BEARING for the NULL-RESULT classification and disqualifying for CONFIRMATION-bundle inclusion (iter-v3/012 adds zero information to the bundle relative to iter-v3/011). The iter-v3/012 catalog row preserves the audit trail but does not contribute a stack ingredient.

## Caveats — iter-v3/013 EXPLORATION-PROMISING-MECHANICAL (NEW SUBTYPE) flags

The +1.0088 IS Sharpe and +2.6970 OOS Sharpe in iter-v3/013 are the **HIGHEST in v3 track history** on both axes; OOS MaxDD 12.47% is the **LOWEST in v3 track history**. HOWEVER the OOS lift is purely mechanical drag-removal — BCH/LDO/TRX OOS trade rosters are byte-identical to iter-v3/012 to 4 decimal places (BCH 31 trades / 38.7% WR / +17.2073 weighted_pnl, LDO 10 trades / 80.0% WR / +40.6020 weighted_pnl, TRX 44 trades / 40.9% WR / +4.0396 weighted_pnl — IDENTICAL across iter-v3/012 and iter-v3/013). Per-symbol architecture means MKR's training was independent of other symbols by construction; the +1.11 OOS Sharpe lift attributes ENTIRELY to removing MKR's −15.49% drag from the aggregate denominator — NOT to any positive interaction effect from 3-symbol Optuna re-optimization. Cataloguing as plain `PROMISING` would mislead future CONFIRMATION QR into bundling drop-MKR as a "new edge ingredient" compoundable with iter-v3/010 labeling + iter-v3/011 z-score in additive fashion, when structurally drop-MKR is a "strictly accretive component decision" (non-compoundable — you can't drop the same symbol twice). The new `EXPLORATION-PROMISING-MECHANICAL` subtype (sister to `NEGATIVE-no-effect` from iter-v3/012) preserves this distinction. The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`subtype = PROMISING-MECHANICAL`** (NEW; sister to `NEGATIVE-no-effect`): trade-roster bit-identity to iter-v3/012 on the 3 retained symbols is the LOAD-BEARING evidence. The +1.11 OOS Sharpe lift is purely mechanical drag-removal, NOT signal discovery. Per-symbol architecture means MKR training was independent of other symbols by construction. Future CONFIRMATION QR distinguishes PROMISING (interaction lift, signal discovery, compoundable across iterations) from PROMISING-MECHANICAL (drag removal, accounting cleanup, NOT compoundable across iterations — you can't drop the same symbol twice). New memory rule `feedback_promising_mechanical_subtype.md` saved per Critic Recommendation 2.

- **`trade_roster_bit_identical_to_iter_v3_012 = TRUE`** for BCH/LDO/TRX (mechanical proof, NOT signal discovery): BCH/LDO/TRX OOS rows IDENTICAL to 4 decimal places between iter-v3/012 and iter-v3/013. Per-symbol architecture means LightGBM trained per-symbol on its own history; MKR's removal touched no other model's parameters. Spot-check on LDO row 5: net 11.07% × 0.88 weight = 9.74 weighted — matches iter-v3/012 exactly. Future CONFIRMATION QR must NOT bundle drop-MKR as a "new edge ingredient" alongside iter-v3/010 labeling + iter-v3/011 z-score in additive fashion.

- **`ldo_concentration_65pct_is_denominator_driven`** (numerator +40.60 IDENTICAL; total grew from 46.36 → 61.85 from MKR's −15.49 removal): the 87.57% → 65.65% concentration improvement is NOT diversification. LDO's 10-trade lottery flag carries forward unchanged from iter-v3/011/012 (8 wins out of 10, 80% WR, exact-binomial 95% CI [44.4%, 97.5%] — too wide to claim signal-from-noise distinction within 95% confidence). The 65.65% concentration figure is still above the 30% per-symbol cap that would apply at CONFIRMATION; future CONFIRMATION QR must scope ex-LDO basket fragility before bundling iter-v3/011 z-score component (carry-forward unchanged).

- **`is_calibration_overshoot_3rd_consecutive`** (predicted [+0.30, +1.20] median +0.65; observed +1.01; 3 consecutive favorable overshoots iter-v3/010, /011, /013): iter-v3/010 (predicted [+0.10, +0.30], observed +0.57 = +0.27 above upper bound), iter-v3/011 (predicted [+0.40, +0.70], observed +0.96 = +0.26 above upper bound), iter-v3/013 (predicted [+0.30, +1.20], observed +1.01 in upper half of band). 3 consecutive favorable IS calibration overshoots establishes a systematic pattern: QR's prior bands have been conservative on IS-axis EXPLORATIONs that DO produce behavioral change at trade-roster level. **Pre-committed**: iter-v3/014's brief Section 7 must widen upper bound on PROMISING predictions for behavior-changing axes by 30% based on this 3-iteration record. iter-v3/012 was the exception (NULL-RESULT, axis was saturated) — does not violate the pattern.

- **`n_high_pbo_cells_99 = 2`** (TRX/2025-10, TRX/2025-11; max-aggregator pre-commit) **+ drop-TRX rule trigger pre-commit (5 consecutive OOS-negative; currently 0/1)**: 2 high-PBO cells in TRX 2025-Q4 carry forward from iter-v3/012; mean aggregator at 0.1075 statistically identical; future CONFIRMATION QR uses `(1 − max_per_cell_pbo)` not just `(1 − mean_pbo)` per audit-trail discipline. Drop-TRX rule does NOT fire (TRX OOS PnL +7.72%, 0/1 OOS-negative trajectory; parallel pre-committed threshold = 5 consecutive OOS-negative iterations to fire a drop-TRX rule analogous to MKR). TRX is the diversifier role. iter-v3/014 brief Section 8 saturation predictor falsifier MUST derive `falsifier_threshold = ceil(1.2 × counterfactual_n_trades)` not hardcoded 240 — replaces fragile constant with per-iteration derivation, robust to single-axis universe variations (per Critic FINAL Recommendation 3).

The five caveats above are NOT disqualifying for catalog inclusion — they are explicit pre-commitments that travel with the catalog row to the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR. iter-v3/013 IS a strictly accretive CONFIRMATION-bundle ingredient (the drop-MKR decision is catalogable and the +1.11 OOS lift is real once MKR is removed) BUT must be treated as a baseline universe decision (always-on at CONFIRMATION), NOT compoundable with iter-v3/010 + iter-v3/011 PROMISING signals as a third axis-lift contributing additively.

## Caveats — iter-v3/014 EXPLORATION-NEGATIVE (clean) flags

The IS Sharpe Δ −0.35 + OOS Sharpe Δ −1.83 in iter-v3/014 represent the LARGEST NEGATIVE OOS delta in v3 track history (mirroring iter-v3/013's +1.11 mechanical drag-removal in opposite direction at 1.65× magnitude). The IS Sharpe Δ −0.35 < −0.10 NEGATIVE threshold AND non-bit-identical roster (153 IS vs iter-v3/013's 209) AND axis propagated (saturation falsifier PASS at 153 < 167; ADX gate fire-rate increased on all 3 symbols by 46.9–66.7%) — all three conditions of brief §4.4 row 5 met cleanly. NOT a NULL-RESULT (distinguishing from iter-v3/012 which had bit-identical roster); NOT PROMISING-MECHANICAL (which requires bit-identical roster on retained symbols). The clean `EXPLORATION-NEGATIVE` classification preserves catalog discipline without subtype proliferation; the asymmetric magnitude observation lives in catalog caveats. The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`verdict = NEGATIVE` (clean)** per brief §4.4 row 5: IS Sharpe Δ −0.35 < −0.10 NEGATIVE threshold AND non-bit-identical roster (153 vs 209) AND axis propagated (saturation falsifier PASS at 153 < 167 derived threshold; ADX kills increased on all 3 symbols 46.9–66.7%). All three §4.4 row 5 conditions met cleanly. NOT a NULL-RESULT (distinguishing from iter-v3/012 which had bit-identical roster); NOT PROMISING-MECHANICAL (which requires bit-identical roster on retained symbols). The clean NEGATIVE classification preserves catalog discipline without subtype proliferation; the asymmetric magnitude observation lives in catalog caveats.

- **`largest_negative_OOS_delta_in_v3_history = -1.83`** (asymmetric magnitude): Δ OOS −1.83 mirrors iter-v3/013's +1.11 in opposite direction at 1.65× magnitude. The ADX axis is asymmetric and high-impact — symmetric-direction validation (iter-v3/015 ADX=18) is MANDATORY per `feedback_adx_axis_asymmetric_v3.md` rule pre-commit. After iter-v3/015 completes, ADX axis is CLOSED for further EXPLORATION; iter-v3/016+ moves to a different axis regardless of iter-v3/015 outcome.

- **`LDO_trade_collapse = 10 → 2`** (−80% kill rate): LDO is the symbol most affected by the ADX tightening (ADX kills +58.5%, the largest proportional increase). iter-v3/013 LDO OOS contributed +40.60% weighted_pnl (65.65% of total); iter-v3/014 LDO OOS contributes only +7.39% (41.13% of total). 50% WR on n=2 is statistically meaningless (exact-binomial 95% CI [1.3%, 98.7%]). The LDO collapse is the dominant OOS Sharpe degradation driver. Future CONFIRMATION QR scoping ex-LDO basket fragility carries forward unchanged from iter-v3/011/012/013 + this iteration's LDO collapse evidence.

- **`oos_n_trades_60_below_130_floor`** (informational caveat): OOS 60 trades is well below the 130-trade floor (`feedback_trade_rate_floor`). Verdict is purely IS-axis driven; OOS Sharpe +0.87 records as informational caveat, NOT in verdict cell. Bundle-level math (60 × 5 outer × 3-4× ensemble = 900-1200 OOS bundle trades) clears the 130-trade floor at CONFIRMATION; single-seed EXPLORATION underpowering is structural, not a methodology issue.

- **`iter-v3/015_axis_mandated = ADX 20 → 18`** per Critic Rec #1: pre-committed via new memory rule `feedback_adx_axis_asymmetric_v3.md`. The asymmetric magnitude (Δ OOS −1.83 = 1.65× iter-v3/013's +1.11) demonstrates ADX is one of the most impactful single-axis variations tested in v3 to date. Cataloguing only the tighter direction without testing the looser direction leaves the catalog with asymmetric understanding. iter-v3/015 ADX=18 closes the symmetric direction; iter-v3/016+ moves to different axis regardless of iter-v3/015 outcome. Cannot be renegotiated post-hoc.

The five caveats above are LOAD-BEARING for the NEGATIVE-clean classification and disqualifying for CONFIRMATION-bundle inclusion (iter-v3/014 demonstrates ADX threshold 25 over-restricts; current value 20 is the structurally-near-optimal baseline pending iter-v3/015 looser-direction symmetric validation). The iter-v3/014 catalog row preserves the audit trail but does not contribute a stack ingredient.

## Caveats — iter-v3/015 EXPLORATION-NEGATIVE-no-effect (NULL-RESULT-class subtype) flags

The IS Sharpe Δ −0.36 + OOS Sharpe +2.1206 (3rd-highest in v3 history but **NOT attributable to new feature**) in iter-v3/015 represent the FIRST NEW feature family attempted in v3 catalog (`tbr_zscore_30` = 30-bar z-score of `taker_buy_quote_volume / quote_volume`, single feature added to V3_FEATURE_COLUMNS, 13 → 14) AND the FIRST iteration whose axis pivot resulted from a user course-correction (catalog banner revoked the prior ADX=18 mandate at setup time, SHA `bd0706b`). Trade roster non-bit-identical (209 → 205 IS, 85 OOS BIT-IDENTICAL to iter-v3/013) AND IS Sharpe Δ −0.36 OUTSIDE §4.4 row 2 PROMISING-INERT band [+0.91, +1.11] by 2.7× band half-width on the unfavorable side AND importance rank 14/14 across all 3 per-symbol models (BCH 16% / LDO 41% / TRX 20% of top feature). NOT a clean NEGATIVE (iter-v3/014 style; would require model using the new constraint), NOT PROMISING-INERT (iter-v3/012 style; would require bit-identical roster + IS Sharpe within band). This is a third pattern: model partially uses feature (4-trade Optuna re-routing) but learns no useful signal (rank 14/14 across all 3 syms). The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`verdict = NEGATIVE-no-effect`** (NULL-RESULT-class subtype; trade roster 209→205 non-bit-identical distinguishes from iter-v3/012's bit-identical NULL-RESULT exemplar): IS Sharpe Δ −0.36 OUTSIDE §4.4 row 2 PROMISING-INERT band [+0.91, +1.11] by 2.7× band half-width on the unfavorable side. Trade roster non-bit-identical (209 → 205 IS) — distinguishes from iter-v3/012's bit-identical NULL-RESULT. Importance rank 14/14 across all 3 syms (BCH 16% / LDO 41% / TRX 20% of top feature) — model demonstrably did not learn feature. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate; feature INERT.

- **`importance_rank = 14/14`** across all 3 per-symbol models (BCH 16% / LDO 41% / TRX 20% of top feature, from `analysis/iteration_v3-015/per_symbol_importance.csv` at QR Round 2 SHA `407954d`): rank-14/14 with the largest gap in the importance distribution between rank 13 and rank 14 confirms the feature is functionally near-discarded. The §4.3 falsifier strictly fires. **BONUS DEFECT discovered**: `_write_feature_importance` at `run_baseline_v3.py:1110-1151` reads only `primary_model_pairs[0]` (BCH last-month inner ensemble), so engineering report's "aggregated across all symbols/months" claim was incorrect. Per-symbol disambiguation required separate analysis script. Pre-committed for iter-v3/016 first commit: aggregate properly OR rename outputs to clarify scope.

- **`IC_orthogonality = clean`** (max |IC| 0.0862 < 0.70 redundancy gate, with `vwap_dev_20`): the feature was structurally well-designed. Failure mode is NOT "candidate correlated to existing features wastes colsample picks" (iter-v2/070 lesson). Failure mode IS "model architecture is the bottleneck — LightGBM trees don't surface microstructure signal even when feature is structurally clean and orthogonal." This diagnostic motivates iter-v3/016's pivot to NEW model architecture (XGBoost head-to-head) rather than continuing to add more microstructure features. Re-introducing more microstructure features (signed-trade imbalance, OFI, cross-features) is deferred until model-architecture finding is established at iter-v3/016 — otherwise we accumulate INERT features that contaminate `colsample_bytree` picks in future iterations.

- **`oos_n_trades_85_below_130_floor`** (informational caveat; OOS bit-identical to iter-v3/013): OOS 85 trades is below the 130-trade floor (`feedback_trade_rate_floor`). Verdict is purely IS-axis driven; OOS Sharpe +2.12 records as informational caveat, NOT in verdict cell. The OOS bit-identity (85 trades, 12.47% MaxDD, 45.23% LDO concentration all matching iter-v3/013 to high precision) is unusually clean evidence that the OOS lift is NOT driven by per-trade economics changes from the new feature — it is mechanical Optuna re-routing on a 14-column loss surface. Bundle-level math (85 × 5 outer × 3-4× ensemble = 1275-1700 OOS bundle trades) clears the floor at CONFIRMATION; single-seed EXPLORATION underpowering is structural, not a methodology issue.

- **`iter-v3/016_axis_mandated = LightGBM → XGBoost head-to-head`** per Critic FINAL Recommendation 1 (new memory rule `feedback_v3_iter016_xgboost_mandate.md`): pre-committed. Drop `tbr_zscore_30` from V3_FEATURE_COLUMNS (back to 13) — restore iter-v3/013 baseline before XGBoost integration. Add `tbr_raw` to V3_NON_FEATURE_COLUMNS (Critic Clarification 4 hygiene). Fix `_write_feature_importance` defect at `run_baseline_v3.py:1110-1151`. Match LightGBM's hyperparam search budget per Optuna config. Cannot be renegotiated post-hoc.

The five caveats above are LOAD-BEARING for the NEGATIVE-no-effect classification and disqualifying for CONFIRMATION-bundle inclusion (iter-v3/015 demonstrates that adding more microstructure features before testing model architecture is premature; LightGBM may be the ceiling for the current feature stack). The iter-v3/015 catalog row preserves the audit trail but does not contribute a stack ingredient.

## Caveats — iter-v3/016 EXPLORATION-NEGATIVE (clean) flags

The IS Sharpe Δ -0.46 + OOS Sharpe Δ **-2.53 (WORST OOS Δ in v3 history)** + OOS MaxDD **40.62% (WORST in v3 history at 4.26× iter-v3/013's 12.47%)** in iter-v3/016 represent the FIRST NEW model architecture attempted in v3 catalog (LightGBM → XGBoost head-to-head on iter-v3/013's 13-feature stack; tbr_zscore_30 dropped as INERT, V3_FEATURE_COLUMNS 14 → 13). Trade roster non-bit-identical (209 → 217 IS, with per-symbol shifts BCH 92→87 = -5; LDO 22→24 = +2; TRX 95→106 = +11 — opposite-direction shifts mask through to portfolio +8 aggregate). §4.4 row 4 NULL-RESULT bit-identity test FAILS unambiguously (per-symbol count equality is necessary for bit-identity, and it fails on every symbol) → row 5 NEGATIVE-clean fires per QR Round 2 Clarification 1 disposition (SHA `06af055`). Per-symbol OOS attribution: TRX +18.82 (only profitable, 258.79% concentration), BCH -2.35, LDO -9.19 — both BCH and LDO turned negative under XGBoost on the SAME 13 features that produced positive results under LightGBM in iter-v3/013. Importance Spearman ρ ≈ 0.56 between LightGBM and XGBoost rank-vectors (sub-hypothesis confirmed but caveat-flagged as may-be-noise per Clarification 2 at n_trials=10 single-seed budget where n_eff=6 indicates sparsely-explored region). The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`verdict = NEGATIVE` (clean)** per brief §4.4 row 5 (per QR Round 2 Clarification 1 disposition): IS Sharpe Δ -0.46 < -0.10 NEGATIVE threshold AND non-bit-identical roster (per-symbol shifts BCH -5, LDO +2, TRX +11 all non-zero — bit-identity falsified by trade-count constraint alone) AND axis propagated (saturation falsifier PASS at 217 < 251 derived threshold; +8 IS trades net). All three §4.4 row 5 conditions met cleanly. NOT a NULL-RESULT (distinguishing from iter-v3/012 which had bit-identical roster); NOT NEGATIVE-no-effect (distinguishing from iter-v3/015 which had non-bit-identical roster but importance rank 14/14 of a single new feature — a "model partially uses but learns no signal" pattern). The clean NEGATIVE classification preserves catalog discipline; the worst-OOS-in-v3-history magnitude observation lives below as separate caveat. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate.

- **`worst_oos_delta_in_v3_history = -2.53`** (vs iter-v3/013 +2.6970 baseline, observed +0.1710): the OOS collapse magnitude exceeds iter-v3/014's -1.83 (previously largest negative OOS delta in v3) by 38%. The structural mechanism: XGBoost depth-wise growth at depth 3-5 with 10 Optuna trials and binary cross-entropy objective produces risk-unaware solutions — IS-PnL maximization without drawdown penalty; the IS/OOS MaxDD ratio of 1.05 (essentially equal) is structurally unusual (typically IS MaxDD > OOS MaxDD because IS Optuna tuning controls drawdown). LightGBM at the same Optuna budget found lower-drawdown solutions because GOSS's gradient sampling implicitly smooths the loss surface. Future XGBoost re-introduction at iter-v3/017+ would require Sharpe-objective Optuna OR drawdown-penalized loss to address this failure mode — neither tested at this iteration.

- **`MaxDD_4x_inflation_worst_in_v3_history`** (40.62% OOS vs iter-v3/013 12.47%; IS 38.69% vs ~22.00%): IS MaxDD also worst-in-v3 at 1.76× iter-v3/013. Per-symbol MaxDD attribution: LDO and BCH both turned negative OOS while LightGBM kept them positive on the same features. The OOS/IS Sharpe ratio of 0.31 is the lowest in v3 track history (substantially below the 0.5 floor that would apply at CONFIRMATION). The IS/OOS MaxDD symmetry (ratio 1.05) signals XGBoost is intrinsically higher-variance under this configuration, not just failing on OOS regime shifts. iter-v3/016 OOS n_trades = 112 below 130 floor is an informational caveat under EXPLORATION; bundle-level math (112 × 5 outer × 3-4× ensemble) clears the floor at CONFIRMATION but the per-symbol concentration (TRX +258.79%) is structurally unacceptable for a CONFIRMATION candidate.

- **`model_arch_axis_closed_scope = "n_trials=10 + cross-entropy objective + depth-wise growth defaults"`** (per Critic Clarification 5 disposition; NOT closed for all XGBoost configurations): three plausible configurations remain untested and should not be foreclosed by this catalog row — (a) **Sharpe-objective Optuna** (custom objective targeting OOS Sharpe instead of IS log-loss), (b) **drawdown-penalized loss** (custom XGBoost objective with drawdown-penalty term subtracted from cross-entropy gradient), (c) **`grow_policy='lossguide'` head-to-head** (XGBoost mimicking LightGBM's leaf-wise growth via lossguide policy — would isolate GOSS-vs-non-GOSS difference from depth-wise-vs-leaf-wise difference; arguably a cleaner architectural ablation). Future iterations could re-test XGBoost in any of these configurations without re-litigating iter-v3/016's NEGATIVE verdict; this catalog row does NOT foreclose. **Importance-divergence finding co-caveated** per Clarification 2: Spearman ρ ≈ 0.56 may be hyperparam-search artifact at n_trials=10 (where `n_eff=6` indicates sparsely-explored search region); structural divergence claim should NOT be propagated as established fact in iter-v3/017+ briefs without multi-seed re-validation (≥5 outer × ≥50 trials).

- **`iter-v3/017_axis_mandated = NEW labeling architecture (meta-labeling preferred per López de Prado AFML Ch. 3; fixed-horizon return labels as fallback)`** per Critic FINAL Recommendation 1: pre-committed via new memory rule `feedback_v3_iter017_metalabeling_mandate.md`. Pre-commits for iter-v3/017 first commit (cannot be renegotiated post-hoc): (a) fix `_write_feature_importance` to aggregate across all walk-forward months — Option A (modify `_train_for_month` to accumulate per-month importances) or Option B (drop OOS importance CSV byte-duplication, rename IS CSV to `model_importance_last_month.csv`) per Clarification 3; (b) restore default `--model lgbm` (XGBoost remains opt-in via `--model xgboost`); (c) ITERATION_LABEL "v3-017"; (d) implement meta-labeling architecture; (e) tighten saturation falsifier band to `[baseline_n_trades × 0.75, baseline_n_trades × 1.25]` per Clarification 4; (f) brief template §4.4 row 5 condition update: "either |Δ| ≥ 11 OR per-symbol shift > 5 trades on any symbol" per Clarification 1.

The five caveats above are LOAD-BEARING for the NEGATIVE-clean classification and disqualifying for CONFIRMATION-bundle inclusion (iter-v3/016 demonstrates that XGBoost depth-wise + cross-entropy + n_trials=10 is risk-unaware; LightGBM remains the v3 baseline architecture pending future XGBoost re-test in different configuration). The iter-v3/016 catalog row preserves the audit trail but does not contribute a stack ingredient.

## Caveats — iter-v3/017 EXPLORATION-NEGATIVE (clean) PATH C sub-flavor flags

The IS Sharpe Δ −0.48 + OOS Sharpe Δ −2.15 (informational, OOS=62 below 130-trade floor) in iter-v3/017 represent the FIRST NEW labeling architecture attempted in v3 catalog (`MetaLabelingStrategy` with M1=LightGBM + M2=LGBMClassifier on iter-v3/013's 13-feature stack; M2 confidence threshold 0.5 PINNED, M2 input vector 14-dim = M1's training-window feature matrix + M1's training-window confidence). M2 demonstrably ACTIVE filtering 42.7% of M1 candidates per-candle (2,976/6,965), but kept trades show no per-trade economics lift — the canonical PATH C sub-flavor (NEGATIVE-over-filter-quality-residual). Trade roster non-bit-identical (209 → 159 IS, with per-symbol shifts BCH 100→73 = −27, LDO 21→16 = −5, TRX 88→70 = −18 — all > 5 threshold). §4.4 row 5 fires unambiguously (IS Sharpe Δ −0.48 < −0.10 AND per-symbol shift > 5 on every symbol AND axis propagated). Label tolerance divergence (brief §2.4 strict TP-first vs impl `pnl > 0`) noted as non-blocking. The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`verdict = NEGATIVE` (clean) — PATH C sub-flavor (NEGATIVE-over-filter-quality-residual)** per brief §4.4 row 5: IS Sharpe Δ −0.48 < −0.10 NEGATIVE threshold AND non-bit-identical roster (per-symbol shifts BCH −27, LDO −5, TRX −18 all > 5) AND axis propagated (M2 demonstrably active 42.7% per-candle veto rate). All three §4.4 row 5 conditions met cleanly. NOT a NULL-RESULT (distinguishing from iter-v3/012 which had bit-identical roster); NOT NEGATIVE-no-effect (distinguishing from iter-v3/015 which had non-bit-identical roster but importance rank 14/14 of a single new feature). PATH C is a NEW sub-flavor specific to over-filter cases where M2 filters aggressively but kept trades produced no per-trade economics lift. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate.

- **`M2_per_candle_veto_rate = 42.7%`** (M2 demonstrably active; 2,976 of 6,965 candidate predictions vetoed): the M2 layer is functionally working — aggressive filtering at threshold 0.5 PINNED. The failure mode is NOT "M2 doesn't activate" — it is "M2 doesn't add quality at this configuration." Kept-trade per-trade economics (IS PF 1.19, OOS PF 1.23, IS WR 30.8%, OOS WR 40.3%) are below iter-v3/013's M1-only baseline at the same per-symbol architecture, demonstrating the M2 layer is correlated with M1's confidence in a way that strips signal alongside noise rather than discriminating. Rules out implementation defects (look-ahead, label leakage, threshold mis-pinning) as the cause of the NEGATIVE outcome.

- **`label_tolerance_divergence = "pnl > 0 vs strict TP-first"`** (non-blocking): brief §2.4 specifies M2=1 iff TP-first-hit-within-timeout; implementation at `metalabeling.py:488` uses `pnl > 0.0` which conflates "TP-hit" with "timeout-with-positive-forward-return". Engineering report's M2 positive-class prior 39.1% vs EDA's pre-registered 33.97% partially reflects this. **Non-blocking because**: (a) divergence disclosed transparently in synthesis.md and brief comment "We use: long_pnl > 0"; (b) qualitative finding (M2 filters 42.7% but kept trades show no quality lift) is robust to either definition; (c) tightening to TP-first-strict would not flip the verdict. Future meta-labeling re-test should use strict TP-first-hit-within-timeout labels per Critic Recommendation 2(a).

- **`n_eff = 4`** (LOWEST in v3 catalog): M2's Optuna search overlaps M1's hyperparameter dimensions, collapsing joint manifold rank under the 30-trial budget (vs iter-v3/013 n_eff=7). The low n_eff means the search explored the joint M1+M2 hyperparam space sparsely; conclusions from this single-seed n_trials=10 budget should be treated as configuration-narrow not architecture-general. PBO=NaN structural under MetaLabelingStrategy: thin walk-forward cells produce zero M1-positive predictions surviving all 7 gates → M2 has no label training data → all-NaN paths → PBO matrix degenerate. Fallback proxy `pbo_frac_positive_paths=0.6444` (29/45 CPCV paths positive). DSR=0.0, PSR=1.0 single-seed-saturation artifacts.

- **`iter-v3/018_axis_mandated = MULTI-SEED VALIDATION OF iter-v3/013 BASELINE`** per Critic FINAL Recommendation 1: pre-committed via new memory rule `feedback_v3_iter018_confirmation_baseline_validation.md`. **iter-v3/018 = first v3 CONFIRMATION = baseline-validation run, NOT bundle assembly**. All 4 PROMISING-class candidates (iter-v3/007 top-13 features, iter-v3/010 ATR labeling 2.0/1.0, iter-v3/011 z-score 2.0, iter-v3/013 drop-MKR universe) are ALREADY cumulatively integrated in the current iter-v3/013 baseline; iter-v3/014-017 were all NEGATIVE/NULL — there is NO NEW ingredient to bundle. The CONFIRMATION question is whether iter-v3/013's IS +1.0088 / OOS +2.6970 holds under multi-seed cross-validation rigor. iter-v3/018 SPEC: `--seeds 2 --n-trials 50` (5 inner × 2 outer = 10 models per cell), ENSEMBLE_SIZE=5, full DSR/PBO/PSR re-evaluation, multi-seed Pareto front for the first time, 4h hard cap per CONFIRMATION cadence rule. Cannot be renegotiated post-hoc.

The five caveats above are LOAD-BEARING for the NEGATIVE-clean PATH C classification and disqualifying for CONFIRMATION-bundle inclusion (iter-v3/017 demonstrates that meta-labeling at threshold 0.5 PINNED + same-feature M2 input + n_trials=10 single-seed budget over-filters without quality lift). The iter-v3/017 catalog row preserves the audit trail but does not contribute a stack ingredient. Future meta-labeling re-test would require strict TP-first labels + DIFFERENT M2 features (non-M1 features) + ≥50 n_trials + multi-seed budget — all four together — to address the failure mode.

## Caveats — iter-v3/019 EXPLORATION-PROMISING-INERT (Falsifier 4 fires) flags

The IS Sharpe Δ +0.78 + OOS Sharpe +0.7847 (informational, 91 trades < 130 floor) in iter-v3/019 represent the FIRST NEW-external-data-source feature family attempted in v3 catalog (`funding_rate_zscore_30` = z-score over rolling 30 funding-cycle window of Binance Futures funding rate; new `funding_v3.py` module + `crypto-trade fetch-funding` CLI + `data/funding_rates/<sym>.csv` cache infrastructure; V3_FEATURE_COLUMNS 13 → 14) AND the FIRST post-bootstrap EXPLORATION (cadence #1 of 10 in the new cycle following iter-v3/018 BOOTSTRAP CONFIRMATION). Falsifier 4 fires unambiguously: importance rank 14/14 across LDO + TRX + Portfolio aggregations; BCH at rank 10/14 (Q3, NOT top-half). Zero symbols clear the rank ≤ 7 (top-half) threshold required for clean-PROMISING. The model demonstrably did not meaningfully learn the funding-rate signal at the n_trials=10 single-seed EXPLORATION budget — same INERT pattern as iter-v3/015 microstructure precedent (`tbr_zscore_30` rank 14/14 across all 3 symbols at the same trial count). The IS Sharpe lift is real in the run but is attributable to hyperparam/colsample noise on a 14-column loss surface, NOT to the funding-rate feature itself. The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`verdict = PROMISING-INERT`** (Falsifier 4 fires; lift NOT attributable to feature): importance rank 14/14 across LDO (3.2% gain share), TRX (3.0% gain share), Portfolio (3.2% gain share). BCH at rank 10/14 (4.2% gain share, Q3 = 71st pctile — above bottom quartile but NOT top-half). Brief §4.4 PROMISING (clean) row requires top-half rank (≤7) for ≥1 symbol; zero symbols clear the bar. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate. Funding-rate axis CLOSED for current 10-EXPLORATION cycle (iter-v3/019–028); retest properly deferred to iter-v3/028+ CONFIRMATION when multiple HIGH-priority axes have accumulated PROMISING evidence and a CONFIRMATION can bundle multiple candidates simultaneously.

- **`single_seed_lottery_suspect = TRUE`** (IS overshoots predicted [+0.45, +0.85] band by +0.31 above upper bound; same pattern as iter-v3/013 falsified at iter-v3/018): predicted IS Sharpe band [+0.45, +0.85] median +0.58. Observed +1.156. The +0.31 IS overshoot above predicted upper bound on a SINGLE-SEED EXPLORATION run is the canonical lottery signature — exactly the same overshoot pattern as iter-v3/013's +1.0088 IS / +2.6970 OOS at single-seed --exploration --n-trials 10 + colsample_bytree=1.0, which was formally FALSIFIED at iter-v3/018 multi-seed CONFIRMATION (62% IS / 86% OOS reduction). Optuna lands in favorable local minimum at n_trials=10 single-seed that does NOT generalize to multi-seed budget. The catalog row flags "single-seed lottery suspect; do NOT bundle pre-multi-seed-validation".

- **`oos_n_trades_91_below_130_floor`** (informational caveat under EXPLORATION; floor applies at CONFIRMATION-bundle level per `feedback_trade_rate_floor_bundle_level`): OOS 91 trades is below the 130-trade floor (`feedback_trade_rate_floor`). Verdict is purely IS-axis driven; OOS Sharpe +0.785 records as informational caveat, NOT in verdict cell. Bundle-level math (91 × 5 outer × 3-4× ensemble = 1365-1820 OOS bundle trades) clears the floor at CONFIRMATION; single-seed EXPLORATION underpowering is structural, not a methodology issue.

- **`dsr_positive_but_exploration_artifact = +0.0167`** (FIRST positive DSR in v3 catalog; n_trials=30 E[max_SR]=2.608 vs observed annualized 4.004 → marginal positive deflation): NOT comparable to BASELINE_V3.md DSR=0.0 at iter-v3/018 (n_trials=1500, observed_SR=1.7, E[max_SR]=3.369). At EXPLORATION mode (--exploration --seeds 1 --n-trials 10), n_trials_per_cell=30 → López de Prado E[max_SR] = √(2 ln 30) = 2.608; observed annualized Sharpe = monthly +1.156 × √12 = 4.004 → marginal positive deflation results when observed_SR > E[max_SR]; +0.0167 is the structural floor of "barely positive" at EXPLORATION trial counts. NOT edge significance evidence. New memory rule `feedback_v3_dsr_mode_artifact.md` enshrines: EXPLORATION-mode DSR/PSR INFORMATIONAL ONLY per the EXPLORATION carve-out; only CONFIRMATION-mode DSR > 0.95 triggers MERGE gate evaluation; iter-v3/019's +0.0167 DSR MUST NOT be cited as edge evidence in catalog rows, briefs, or future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR.

- **`iter-v3/020_axis_mandated = HIGH-priority #2 concentration architecture`** per Critic FINAL Recommendation #1 (review SHA `1bc6028`) + `feedback_v3_iter019_axis_priorities.md` LOCKED 2026-05-07: pre-committed. Either (a) hard `max_per_symbol_pnl_share = 0.40` portfolio constraint at the aggregation layer, OR (b) universe expansion to 5+ symbols to dilute concentration mechanically. **DROP `funding_rate_zscore_30` from V3_FEATURE_COLUMNS at iter-v3/020 setup commit (revert 14 → 13)** for clean iter-v3/020 attribution surface. **KEEP** `funding_v3.py` module + `crypto-trade fetch-funding` CLI + `data/funding_rates/<sym>.csv` cache infrastructure (zero revert cost; preserves option for iter-v3/028+ CONFIRMATION retest without re-implementing the fetcher). Cannot be renegotiated post-hoc.

The five caveats above are LOAD-BEARING for the PROMISING-INERT classification and disqualifying for CONFIRMATION-bundle inclusion (iter-v3/019 demonstrates that NEW feature families at n_trials=10 EXPLORATION budget produce rank 14/14 regardless of feature quality — iter-v3/015 + iter-v3/019 calibrate this prior). The iter-v3/019 catalog row preserves the audit trail but does not contribute a stack ingredient. Future funding-rate retest at iter-v3/028+ CONFIRMATION would consume ~5h wall-clock and is properly deferred until other HIGH-priority axes have accumulated PROMISING-class evidence.

## Caveats — iter-v3/021 EXPLORATION-NEGATIVE (clean) flags

The IS Sharpe Δ -0.06 + OOS Sharpe Δ **-0.83 (WORST OOS Δ on iter-v3/018 anchor basis in v3 catalog history)** in iter-v3/021 represent the FIRST universe-expansion EXPLORATION in v3 catalog (V3_MODELS 3 → 5; +HBARUSDT +AVAXUSDT; HIGH-priority axis #2b; orthogonal-mechanism alternative to iter-v3/020 per-symbol cap per `feedback_v3_concentration_is_signal.md` LOCKED). Trade roster non-bit-identical (172 → 311 IS trades; 5 sym ≠ 3 sym universe). §4.4 row 5 fires unambiguously: IS Sharpe Δ -0.06 within predicted band BUT OOS Sharpe -0.4380 < anchor -0.10 threshold (anchor +0.2869); roster non-identity (5 sym ≠ 3 sym); saturation falsifier FIRES at 311 IS trades > 269 upper bound — axis effect was LARGER than predicted but all excess trades unprofitable. Trade-rate floor cleared at bundle level (144 ≥ 130) — the QR's 2-symbol pre-decision reasoning was directionally correct on volume but fundamentally wrong on edge. The five caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline so the future CONFIRMATION-bundling QR inherits the rule set without re-deriving any of them under post-hoc pressure:

- **`verdict = NEGATIVE` (clean)** per brief §4.4 row 5: IS Sharpe Δ -0.06 within predicted [+0.30, +0.55] lower bound BUT OOS Sharpe -0.4380 < anchor -0.10 threshold by -0.7249 AND non-bit-identical roster (5 sym ≠ 3 sym universe; per-symbol shifts on incumbents BCH/LDO/TRX all non-zero) AND axis propagated (saturation falsifier FIRES at 311 IS trades > 269 upper bound). All three §4.4 row 5 conditions met cleanly. NOT a NULL-RESULT (distinguishing from iter-v3/012 which had bit-identical roster); NOT NEGATIVE-no-effect (distinguishing from iter-v3/015 which had non-bit-identical roster but importance rank 14/14 of a single new feature). **Worst OOS Δ on iter-v3/018 anchor basis at -0.83**; prior worst -2.53 (iter-v3/016 XGBoost) was on a different baseline level (iter-v3/013 single-seed). The clean NEGATIVE classification preserves catalog discipline; the worst-OOS observation lives below as separate caveat. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate.

- **`new_symbols_both_negative_IS_drag = -85.7%_combined`** (HBAR -36.5% + AVAX -49.2% IS weighted_pnl): both HBAR and AVAX are net drag IS+OOS at single-seed EXPLORATION budget. EDA's "lowest mean |corr|" metric (HBAR 0.41, AVAX 0.50) captured **price-level return diversity NOT signal diversity in the 13-feature statistical-moments space**. The 13-feature stack identified existing-symbol patterns through iter-v3/007-013 evolution; HBAR and AVAX have different mid-cap altcoin return regimes that LightGBM at n_trials=35 split 5 ways cannot fit. **HBAR + AVAX axis CLOSED at catalog level** for current 10-EXPLORATION cycle — cannot revisit without fundamentally different mechanism (e.g., dedicated multi-seed CONFIRMATION budget for the 5-symbol universe; or new feature family designed against HBAR/AVAX-specific microstructure). Other symbol candidates (ATOM/FIL/ALGO) are deferred to future LOW-priority retest only AFTER higher-priority structural axes are exhausted.

- **`bch_oos_turned_negative_first_time = -10.47_weighted_pnl`** (incumbent symbol regression as side effect of universe expansion): BCH weighted_pnl OOS -10.47 (was strongly positive at iter-v3/018 anchor) is the dominant OOS Sharpe degradation driver alongside LDO -13.24 (lottery flag carried forward). Mechanism: 5-symbol training context shifted Optuna's hyperparameter selection at n_trials=35 toward regions that overfit BCH's IS pattern — a side effect of the broader cell-level Optuna search at lower per-symbol budget (175 fits ÷ 5 sym = 35 fits/symbol on incumbents). Per-symbol architecture preserves model-fit independence at the prediction layer but NOT at the Optuna search-budget layer. Future universe-expansion EXPLORATIONs should test 1-symbol expansion at a time to isolate per-symbol contribution OR be deferred to CONFIRMATION budget where per-symbol fit quality is non-degraded.

- **`saturation_falsifier_FIRES_311_over_269_upper_bound`** (axis propagated stronger than predicted): brief §2.3 predicted IS trade band [186, 269] under 3-sym × (5/3) proportional scaling with ±18% saturation. Observed 311 = +42 trades over upper bound. The new symbols emit MORE trade volume than the proxy ratio predicted (likely because HBAR/AVAX have higher 8h candle volatility producing more z-score / ADX / Hurst gate-passes), but **all excess trades are unprofitable**. Symmetric to iter-v3/014's ADX-axis falsifier behavior: the axis propagated cleanly, but the propagation direction was wrong. The §2.3 saturation predictor formula `falsifier_threshold = ceil(1.2 × counterfactual_n_trades)` per `feedback_axis_saturation_predictor.md` correctly fired the falsifier; the rule continues to operate as designed.

- **`iter-v3/022_axis_mandated = TRX/2022-Q4 regime gate (MEDIUM #4 ELEVATED)`** per Critic FINAL Recommendation #1 (review SHA `f5b89a3`) + iter-v3/021 diary lessons (a)-(d): pre-committed via diary `Pre-Commit for iter-v3/022` section. Surgical structural fix addressing BASELINE_V3.md outstanding constraint #5 (PBO max=1.0 on TRX/2022-10 + TRX/2023-01 — FTX/LUNA crash regime). Implementation: regime-conditional kill switch for TRX position-taking when (a) BTC_drawdown_30d > 30% over 30 days, OR (b) BTC_volatility_zscore > 2.5. Per `feedback_v3_concentration_is_signal.md`, regime-conditional kill switch is one of 4 permitted orthogonal mechanisms (binary off/on) for future structural-architecture EXPLORATION. **iter-v3/022 first commit pre-commits**: (1) DROP HBARUSDT + AVAXUSDT from V3_MODELS (revert 5 → 3); (2) REQUIRED_GAP back 110 → 66 = (21+1) × 3; (3) Add `regime_gate` config field to RiskV2Config (default disabled outside this iteration); (4) Implement BTC_drawdown_30d + BTC_volatility_zscore tracker in RiskV2Wrapper; (5) ITERATION_LABEL "v3-022"; (6) Per-symbol cap stays disabled (iter-v3/020 closed it); (7) Test for past-only discipline on regime gate. **NEW memory rule `feedback_v3_universe_expansion_eda_insufficient.md`** committed (per diary lesson (a)). Cannot be renegotiated post-hoc.

The five caveats above are LOAD-BEARING for the NEGATIVE-clean classification and disqualifying for CONFIRMATION-bundle inclusion (iter-v3/021 demonstrates that universe expansion at EXPLORATION budget with 35 fits/symbol on new entries is over-stretched relative to incumbent symbols' BOOTSTRAP CONFIRMATION fit quality of 500 fits/symbol). The iter-v3/021 catalog row preserves the audit trail but does not contribute a stack ingredient. Future universe-expansion retest with ATOM/FIL/ALGO would require either dedicated multi-seed CONFIRMATION budget OR 1-symbol-at-a-time isolation strategy — neither tested at this iteration.

## Caveats — iter-v3/074 EXPLORATION-INERT-AT-EXPLORATION flags

iter-v3/074 (cycle 2 #4 of 10) tested **primitive 9 — a regime-conditional kill switch** (`enable_regime_gate=True`, `regime_gate_symbols=("TRXUSDT",)`; suppresses TRX entries when BTC drawdown_30d > 20% OR |vol_z_30d| > 1.5). It was chosen specifically as a **holding-time-ORTHOGONAL** axis to satisfy the Critic /073 hard constraint after three consecutive holding-time-EXTENSION SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT iterations (/065, /071, /073). Verdict INERT-AT-EXPLORATION per brief Section 8.3; Critic FINAL `2371324` OVERALL=MERGE certifies the classification clean (NOT an advancement). Anchor = iter-v3/060 EXPLORATION-MODE-REFERENCE (IS +0.8325 / OOS +0.1403). The caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline:

- **`verdict = INERT-AT-EXPLORATION`** — both shifts inside the noise bands (IS Δ +0.013 ∈ ±0.10; OOS Δ +0.069 ∈ ±0.20) AND SUSPICIOUS does not fire (OOS/IS ratio 0.247 < 3.0; OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode does not fire — OOS shift +0.069 < +0.20). NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate. The regime gate suppressed only **3 IS + 5 OOS** TRX trades — too few to move headline metrics.

- **`null_result_ruled_out = TRUE`** — brief Section 8.5 defines NULL-RESULT strictly as `regime_gate_fires total = 0`. The gate DID fire (3 IS + 5 OOS suppressions per the Gate Efficacy table), so NULL-RESULT is excluded by its own definition; INERT is the LOCKED-criteria-correct classification (Critic adversarial Q3).

- **`holding_time_orthogonal_hypothesis = CONFIRMED`** — TRX kept-roster duration delta = −0.389 candles IS / −0.240 OOS — near-zero and slightly NEGATIVE, the OPPOSITE direction from the /065/071/073 holding-time-extension family, well inside the +1.5-candle falsifier. OOS/IS ratio 0.247 (vs the extension family's 1.56 / 0.68 / 6.85). This confirms `feedback_v3_is_oos_regime_divergence.md`: holding-time-extension axes load the v3 IS/OOS regime factor; holding-time-orthogonal axes do not.

- **`bch_bit_identical_to_060 = TRUE`** — the gate `regime_gate_symbols=("TRXUSDT",)` is TRX-scoped; BCH cannot be touched. QE verified BCH 37/37 OOS trades field-by-field identical to /060 (`comparison.csv` line 18: BCH +1.9078 / 37 trades). LDO likewise untouched. Single-axis discipline verified — the positive control.

- **`engineering_report_ratio_forensic_error`** (non-blocking; Critic Rec #1) — the /074 engineering report cited prior SUSPICIOUS OOS/IS ratios as "5.04/4.51/12.55" for /065/071/073; these reconcile to NO source artifact and the "/073 = 12.55" entry directly contradicts /073's byte-confirmed `comparison.csv` ratio 6.8468. The CORRECTED within-iteration `comparison.csv` `monthly_sharpe` ratios are **/065 = 1.56, /071 = 0.68, /073 = 6.85** (recorded in `diary-v3/iteration_v3-074.md` Section 4). **Canonical definition fixed:** "OOS/IS ratio" = the within-iteration `comparison.csv` `monthly_sharpe` ratio column (the value the Section 8.4 SUSPICIOUS gate consumes). Future briefs, engineering reports, and diaries must use this and not introduce alternative constructions.

- **`regime_gate_axis_CLOSED_two_data_points`** (Critic Rec #2) — primitive 9 produced NEGATIVE-pre-fix at /022 and INERT-post-fix at /074. The post-fix re-evaluation cleanly discharged the `feedback_v3_walkforward_lookahead_bug.md` re-eval eligibility for this axis. Added to BASELINE_V3.md "Dead Ideas" as tested-twice-no-signal — not to be re-proposed in cycle 2 or cycle 3.

- **`iter-v3/075_axis_guidance`** (Critic Rec #3) — cycle-2 #5 must target the IS bear/chop drag directly (brief EDA PART 1 localized it: IS bear/chop monthly Sharpe −0.0242, 27.8% positive months) with a holding-time-orthogonal mechanism that acts on the FULL roster (the /074 kill switch touched too few trades — 3 IS / 5 OOS — to lift the drag). The /075 brief must pre-register a behavioral-effect predictor estimating a materially larger trade-population effect. Suggested direction: a NEW feature family discriminating bear vs bull regimes within the IS window.

DSR (legacy) = 0.0 and DSR_relative_B4 = 0.0715 FAIL their 0.95 thresholds but are INFORMATIONAL at EXPLORATION per `feedback_v3_dsr_mode_artifact.md` (EXPLORATION-mode `n_trials=315` is not comparable to CONFIRMATION-mode `n_trials=1050`). PSR = 0.9985, PBO = 0.1278 PASS, `frac_positive_paths = 0.6444` PASS.

## Caveats — iter-v3/075 EXPLORATION-INERT-AT-EXPLORATION flags

iter-v3/075 (cycle 2 #5 of 10) tested **primitive 12 — a BTC-trend-regime position-SIZE de-rate scalar** (`enable_regime_size_scalar=True`, `regime_size_scalar_symbols=("LDOUSDT","TRXUSDT")`; when BTC is in bear/chop, `close[t-1] < SMA_270(close)[t-1]`, the position WEIGHT of LDO/TRX trades is multiplied by 0.50; BCH + bull-regime trades unchanged). It was chosen as a **holding-time-ORTHOGONAL, full-roster** axis to satisfy the Critic /074 hard constraint (Rec #3): target the IS bear/chop drag with a mechanism that re-weights EVERY bear/chop trade, not a narrow stress-bar subset like /074's 3-IS/5-OOS kill switch. Verdict INERT-AT-EXPLORATION per brief Section 8; Critic FINAL `2211927` OVERALL=MERGE certifies the classification clean AND adjudicates the OOS-tuning remediation COMPLETE (NOT an advancement). Anchor = iter-v3/060 EXPLORATION-MODE-REFERENCE (IS +0.8325 / OOS +0.1403). The caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline:

- **`verdict = INERT-AT-EXPLORATION`** — IS Δ +0.1198 clears the +0.10 PROMISING IS floor, BUT OOS Δ -0.0768 fails the +0.20 PROMISING OOS floor and sits inside the [-0.20,+0.20] noise band (above the -0.20 NEGATIVE floor). Per `feedback_v3_strict_both_is_oos_baseline.md`, BOTH axes must clear — the strong-IS-only result does NOT rescue PROMISING. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate.

- **`oos_tuning_defect_and_correction`** (PROCESS — the iteration's process story) — the QR's first EDA pass (`9a04f6f`) selected the de-rate scalar by an **OOS-tuning rule**: T3/T8 computed a per-candidate OOS counterfactual and `main()` filtered candidates on `oos_delta >= -0.20` and ranked survivors by `oos_delta` — tuning a design parameter on OOS data, a direct `feedback_no_cheating.md` violation; the first brief text claimed "IS-only" while the committed code contradicted it. The orchestrator caught the defect **PRE-GATE** (before Phase 5.5, before any backtest) and sent the QR back. Correction: the de-rate `0.50` became an **a-priori, data-free default** (a hardcoded literal — "halve size in an adverse BTC regime" — not the output of any sort/filter/argmax on IS or OOS data); T3/T8 became IS-only (every `oos_delta`/`oos_monthly_sharpe`/`oos_is_ratio` column removed — corrected EDA `a254e5a`); brief Sections 2.4/4/7 rewritten mechanism-derived and Section 10.0 disclosed the correction (corrected brief `a41d308`). The Critic independently audited the corrected EDA and adjudicated the remediation **COMPLETE** — judging the a-priori `0.50` genuinely un-overfittable (a round-number "half size" cut is about as un-tunable as a parameter gets; the T8 IS lift is monotone in aggressiveness so the only IS-fittable optimum is the boundary 0.25, which the QR explicitly rejected fitting to). The defect was caught and fully remediated before any data was generated. Lessons (Critic Recs #1, #2): (a) a correction sweep must cover ALL textual references — code comments included; a stale `risk_v2.py:182-183` comment was missed and fixed post-hoc by the orchestrator at `7c5c7a3`; (b) the no-OOS-tuning rule is codified — every future EDA script must select each design parameter via a function whose inputs are demonstrably IS-only or a-priori, with the EDA docstring stating per parameter the exact selection function and its input columns (a mandatory EDA section, per the corrected /075 EDA's template).

- **`key_finding_IS_up_OOS_down_tension`** — Primitive 12 lifted IS +0.1198 but cost OOS -0.0768. This is STRUCTURAL, not noise: a BTC-trend classifier that de-rates the IS bear/chop drag NECESSARILY also de-rates OOS-uptrend LDO/TRX trades, because v3's IS window (2022-09 → 2025-03) is bear/chop-heavy and the OOS window (2025-03 → 2026-05) is a persistent uptrend — the SAME "bear/chop" tag suppresses IS-bleeding trades AND OOS-rewarding trades. The de-rate trades IS for OOS roughly 1:1. This is DISTINCT from the /065/071/073 holding-time-extension regime-divergence trap (those lengthened duration; /075 is holding-time-orthogonal — see next bullet). It is a post-gate macro BTC-trend classifier whose sign is regime-correlated with the IS/OOS split. This is the cycle-2 #6 (/076) design problem.

- **`holding_time_orthogonal = CONFIRMED`** — LDO+TRX kept-roster mean trade duration Δ = 0.000 IS / +0.004 OOS candles (the +0.004 is entirely the one extra data-extent trade; 250× below the +1.0 falsifier). A size scalar deletes no trade — holding-time-orthogonality CONFIRMED. OOS/IS monthly Sharpe ratio = 0.0667 — healthy, ≪ 3.0 SUSPICIOUS gate; no regime-divergence signature (consistent with the /074 finding that holding-time-orthogonal axes do not load the v3 IS/OOS regime factor).

- **`bch_bit_identical_to_060 = TRUE`** — the de-rate scope `("LDOUSDT","TRXUSDT")` excludes BCH. QE verified BCH **73/73 IS + 37/37 OOS** trades field-by-field byte-identical to /060; Critic Check 7 independently confirmed `weight_factor` and `weighted_pnl` byte-identity on the BCH rows. Single-axis discipline verified — the positive control.

- **`behavioral_effect_predictor`** — brief Section 4.4 pre-registered ≈24 IS / ≈32 OOS LDO+TRX trades de-rated; observed 28 IS / 33 OOS (trade-level), within the "approximate" band (the T5 derivation used the static /060 roster without 3-seed Optuna variation) and materially larger than /074's 3-IS/5-OOS — the brief's mandate satisfied. Signal-level `regime_size_scalar_fires` = 237 (BCH 0 — positive control, LDO 5, TRX 232) → NULL-RESULT ruled out (the scalar fired).

- **`iter-v3/076_axis_guidance`** (Critic Rec #3) — cycle-2 #6 must be chosen to BREAK the IS-up/OOS-down tension. Primitive 12 demonstrated a post-gate macro BTC-trend classifier de-rating the IS drag is OOS-costly by construction (the discriminator's sign is regime-correlated with the IS/OOS split). The /076 brief must pre-register (committed IS-only analysis) whether a mechanism exists that discriminates IS bear/chop from OOS uptrend WITHOUT a feature whose sign is regime-correlated that way — e.g. a feature-internal IS-regime discriminator the *model* can learn, rather than a post-gate macro classifier. Cycle 2 is 0/5 clean PROMISING.

DSR (legacy) = 0.0, PSR = 0.798, and DSR_relative_B4 ≈ 0.0001 (5.3e-05) FAIL their 0.95 thresholds but are INFORMATIONAL at EXPLORATION per `feedback_v3_dsr_mode_artifact.md` (EXPLORATION-mode `n_trials=315` is not comparable to CONFIRMATION-mode `n_trials=1050`). PBO = 0.1278 PASS (bit-identical to /060 — post-gate weight scalar leaves the Optuna landscape untouched), `frac_positive_paths = 0.644` PASS.

## Caveats — iter-v3/076 EXPLORATION-SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT flags

iter-v3/076 (cycle 2 #6 of 10) tested **a NEW feature, `range_efficiency_50`** — a Kaufman-style unsigned 50-bar path-efficiency measure (`|close[t]-close[t-50]| / sum(|close.diff()|, 50)`, clipped [0,1], `.shift(1)`) added to `V3_FEATURE_COLUMNS` as the 15th feature. It was chosen as a sign-invariant feature-internal IS-regime discriminator the model learns, intended to BREAK the /075 IS-up/OOS-down tension (Critic /075 Rec #3). The feature is MATHEMATICALLY IDENTICAL to the dead-code, literal-name-banned `efficiency_ratio_50` (/043 DISASTROUS) — a rule-sanctioned re-evaluation. Verdict SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT per brief Section 8; Critic FINAL `8203410` OVERALL=MERGE certifies the classification clean AND adjudicates the /043 banned-feature re-evaluation a LEGITIMATE rule-sanctioned re-eval (NOT an advancement). Anchor = iter-v3/060 EXPLORATION-MODE-REFERENCE (IS +0.8325 / OOS +0.1403). The caveats below are catalogued for audit-trail discipline:

- **`verdict = SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT`** — BOTH SUSPICIOUS gates fire simultaneously: (1) the ratio gate — OOS/IS monthly Sharpe ratio = +0.6489 / +0.0431 = **15.0422** ≫ 3.0, the highest in cycle 2 (above /071's 4.51 and /073's 6.85); (2) the OOS-dominant sub-mode — IS shift -0.7894 < 0 AND OOS shift +0.5086 ≥ +0.20. Per the Section 8 disjunctive order (SUSPICIOUS → NULL-RESULT → NEGATIVE → PROMISING → INERT), SUSPICIOUS takes precedence over NEGATIVE-IS (which would also fire at IS Δ -0.79 < -0.10) with no magnitude qualifier. The IS edge was DESTROYED — IS Sharpe collapsed to +0.0431, IS total PnL +51.89 → +3.00, IS MaxDD blew out 31.87% → 61.69%. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate; SUSPICIOUS axes never advance.

- **`hypothesis_falsified — marginal vs conditional orthogonality`** (the key scientific finding) — the brief's Section 1 / 10.2 thesis was that `range_efficiency_50`, being sign-invariant and (per EDA T3) regime-orthogonal at |regime-sign corr| = 0.010, is "structurally unable to produce the /071/073 SUSPICIOUS signature"; Section 7 weighted SUSPICIOUS at ≈3%. The observed result IS SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT (ratio 15.04). The lesson (Critic Rec #1): **marginal regime-orthogonality ≠ conditional orthogonality.** T3 measured only the feature's *marginal* value-distribution correlation with the calendar regime label. It does NOT bound how LightGBM *conditionally* uses the feature in interaction with the other 14 directional features. The model's conditional use produced regime-correlated trade selection. The "structurally unable" claim conflated marginal feature orthogonality (necessary, T3-measured, true: 0.010) with conditional model orthogonality (the binding property, NOT T3-measured, false). Corrective: future "regime-orthogonal feature" briefs must address conditional orthogonality — a concrete proxy is to correlate the candidate feature's model-split-allocation / SHAP attribution (NOT the raw feature value) with the regime label.

- **`holding_time_forensic — regime factor loaded via trade SELECTION`** — the >+1.0-candle holding-time falsifier did NOT fire (IS full-roster mean duration Δ +0.005, OOS Δ +0.480, both below +1.0). But the +0.480 OOS shift is ROSTER-COMPOSITION-driven: the 62 common OOS trades (matched on `(symbol, open_time)`) have a duration delta of exactly **0.000** — identical barriers, identical entries, identical exits (mechanically forced; ATR multipliers `(2.0,1.0)` and 21-candle timeout unchanged; Critic verified on sampled common trades). The +0.480 comes from composition: the 40 ADDED OOS trades have mean duration **7.25 candles** vs the 40 REMOVED at **6.03**. The feature changed WHICH trades the model selects, skewing the OOS roster toward longer-held trades — and in the OOS sustained uptrend, longer-held trades score HIGH on path efficiency. This is how a feature with NO barrier-extension mechanism still loaded the regime factor. The QR brief Section 4.3 "~0 duration change" predictor was a DEFECT — it considered only per-trade barrier mechanics, not the roster-composition channel. Corrective (Critic Rec #2): the holding-time predictor must pre-register an added-vs-removed trade-set mean-duration sub-channel falsifier, not only the >+1.0-candle full-roster shift.

- **`/043 re-evaluation = LEGITIMATE rule-sanctioned re-eval; axis CLOSED across 2 data points`** — `range_efficiency_50` is bit-identical math to `efficiency_ratio_50` (Critic verified: same Kaufman formula, 50-bar window, `1e-9` epsilon, clip, shift, fillna). The re-introduction is rule-sanctioned: `feedback_v3_walkforward_lookahead_bug.md` lists iter-v3/029-/057 (incl. /043) as eligible for post-walk-forward-fix re-evaluation; the math identity was disclosed in 5 places; the literal `efficiency_ratio_50` name + its runtime `RuntimeError` ban + the dispatch-removal all stayed intact (the banned column is never generated) — honest naming, not name-laundering. The re-eval did NOT vindicate the feature: IS collapsed to +0.0431 (MaxDD 61.69%); the feature produced SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT (a different failure mode from /043's bilateral -0.84/-0.90, but a failure). The Kaufman path-efficiency axis is CLOSED across two data points (/043 pre-fix DISASTROUS + /076 post-fix SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT); the `feedback_v3_walkforward_lookahead_bug.md` re-evaluation eligibility for this axis is DISCHARGED → added to BASELINE_V3.md Dead Ideas. Do NOT re-propose `efficiency_ratio_50` / `range_efficiency_50`.

- **`section_7_calibration_miss`** (Critic Rec #3) — the brief's ≈3% SUSPICIOUS weight (the lowest of any cycle-2 brief) was a calibration miss: a 3% weight while the empirical cycle-2 SUSPICIOUS base rate is 50% (3 of 6 EXPLORATIONs SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT) is a mechanism-story override of the data — the brief allowed a marginal-orthogonality narrative to push the probability ~17× below the running base rate. Corrective: future briefs' Section 7 SUSPICIOUS probability must be floored near the running cycle base rate unless the brief presents a *conditional*-orthogonality proof strong enough to justify deviating below it.

- **`feature_was_learned — INERT and NULL-RESULT both ruled out`** — `range_efficiency_50` importance ranks 15/12/10 for BCH/LDO/TRX (NOT rank-15/15 for all → INERT importance falsifier does NOT fire); 100 IS trades added / 90 removed (NOT bit-identical → NULL-RESULT does NOT fire). The feature was substantially learned; the IS collapse is a genuine model-behavior outcome, not a wiring bug. Max |IC| of `range_efficiency_50` vs the 14 baseline features = 0.2157 (with `sym_vs_btc_ret_7d`) — Category 1 off-the-shelf indicator, strict |IC|<0.70 gate applies, comfortable PASS; the feature is genuinely orthogonal in the marginal sense.

- **`iter-v3/077_axis_guidance`** (Critic Rec #1, #3) — cycle-2 #7 axis is QR-EDA-driven. If a NEW "regime-orthogonal" feature is proposed, the /077 EDA must pre-register a committed CONDITIONAL-orthogonality test (model-split-allocation / SHAP attribution correlated with the regime label) — a near-zero marginal correlation is necessary but NOT sufficient. The Kaufman path-efficiency axis is CLOSED — do not re-propose. BASELINE_V3.md cycle-2 priority #1 (meta-labeling) has still not been cleanly executed; a re-scoped M2 verified BOTH holding-time-orthogonal AND conditionally-orthogonal remains the highest-ranked structural axis.

DSR (legacy) = 0.0, PSR = 1.0, DSR_relative_B4 ≈ 1.0 (0.999995) — Check-3 edge axes INFORMATIONAL at EXPLORATION per `feedback_v3_dsr_mode_artifact.md` (EXPLORATION-mode `n_trials=315` is not comparable to CONFIRMATION-mode `n_trials=1050`). PBO = 0.0974 PASS (the one BLOCK-triggering Check-3 axis at EXPLORATION; improved from /060's 0.1278 — a model that selects better OOS trades shows less per-cell overfitting in OOS paths even as IS collapses). `frac_positive_paths = 0.6444` PASS (CPCV invariant, identical to /060).
