## Caveats — iter-v3/077 EXPLORATION-INERT-AT-EXPLORATION flags

iter-v3/077 (cycle 2 #7 of 10) is a **PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC** — the axis is report instrumentation only: a new runner function `_write_conditional_orthogonality()` emitting `conditional_orthogonality.csv` (the Critic /076 Rec #1 instrument). Mandatory secondary edit: REVERT /076's `range_efficiency_50` (`V3_FEATURE_COLUMNS` 15→14, restoring the /060 anchor stack). The brief Section 4.1 PREDICTED the trade roster would be bit-identical to /060 (intended NULL-RESULT). Verdict INERT-AT-EXPLORATION per brief Section 8.3; Critic FINAL `46e9fe9` OVERALL=MERGE certifies the classification clean (NOT an advancement). Anchor = iter-v3/060 EXPLORATION-MODE-REFERENCE.

- **`verdict = INERT-AT-EXPLORATION`** — both shifts inside the noise bands (IS Δ -0.0089 ∈ ±0.10; OOS Δ +0.0675 ∈ ±0.20) AND SUSPICIOUS does not fire (OOS/IS ratio 0.2523 ≪ 3.0; OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode does not fire — OOS shift +0.0675 < +0.20) AND the roster is NOT bit-identical to /060. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate — a PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC produces no edge ingredient. The `conditional_orthogonality.csv` instrumentation lands as accretive report tooling.

- **`bit_identity_prediction_falsified — benign, fully root-caused`** — the brief Section 4.1 over-claimed bit-identity as "an algebraic identity." The IS `(symbol, open_time)` KEY roster IS bit-identical to /060 (159=159, 0 added, 0 removed; `per_symbol.csv` byte-identical). The divergence is 13 TRX IS `weight_factor` values floored at exactly 0.5 — root cause `vol_scale_floor_per_symbol={"TRXUSDT": 0.5}`, introduced at **iter-v3/061** (16 iterations after /060, in-code provenance comment at `run_baseline_v3.py:1689-1693`) — genuine code accretion EXTERNAL to /077's config. The OOS +1 trade is the data-extent artifact (LDO `end_of_data` ~2026-05-14 + one TRX OOS trade resolving `end_of_data`→`take_profit`). Not a wiring defect (Critic Adjudication #1). The brief's own Section 8.3 pre-registered this exact INERT contingency (≈4% benign-perturbation tail), so the classifier absorbed the miss cleanly — the over-confident point-estimate framing is a process defect (Critic Rec #2), NOT a BLOCK.

- **`anchor_staleness — the load-bearing methodology finding`** — /077 is the FIRST iteration since /060 to run the exact /060 14-feature config with no axis. It does NOT reproduce the frozen /060 anchor. The current-code /060-config baseline is **IS +0.8236 / OOS +0.2078** (frozen anchor was +0.8325 / +0.1403). Exact additive decomposition: IS code-drift **-0.0089** (the iter-v3/061 TRX `vol_scale_floor` — permanent, deterministic, fixed offset) + OOS data-extent **+0.0675** (the 2026-05 OOS month post-dating /060's data fetch — grows monotonically with calendar time). /071-/076 all computed deltas against the frozen +0.8325/+0.1403; the Critic verified the /071-/076 CLASSIFICATIONS are ROBUST (SUSPICIOUS by the ratio gate at 4.51/6.85/15.04; INERT by noise-band shifts — neither flips under a ±0.07 drift) and the cycle-2 CONFIRMATION is UNAFFECTED (it anchors on BASELINE_V3.md /059, not /060). **See the dedicated anchor-staleness cycle-note below.**

- **`reframing_finding — the IS drag is mis-localized`** — EDA T1 (`T1_regime_stratification.csv`) stratifies /060's IS monthly PnL by a clean per-calendar-month BTC-regime label (a month is BULL if ≥50% of BTC 8h bars have `close[t-1] > SMA_270[t-1]`, `.shift(1)` past-only). Result: IS_BEAR_CHOP Sharpe **+1.2909** (15 months, 47% positive) vs IS_BULL Sharpe **+0.4100** (18 months, 33% positive). **The IS drag is in the BULL months, NOT bear/chop.** /074, /075, /076 all targeted "the IS bear/chop drag" using the strategy's own ENDOGENOUS trade-level regime tag (partially circular). The Critic (Adjudication #3) certified the per-month BTC-regime label methodologically superior (exogenous to the strategy). Three cycle-2 iterations optimized against the wrong stratum. EDA T6 escapability synthesis: win/loss duration ratio 2.08, 64% stop-loss exits, bull-month WR 32.7% vs bear/chop 43.6% — the IS problem is a directional-quality / entry-discrimination problem in bull regimes. **See the reframing cycle-note below.**

- **`conditional_orthogonality_deliverable`** — `conditional_orthogonality.csv` is the Critic /076 Rec #1 tool, now built. PART A is the runner's last-walk-forward-month gain-importance share (degenerate single-point map — supplementary); PART B is the full per-IS-month map copied verbatim from the committed EDA `T3_conditional_orthogonality.csv` (Critic verified byte-match). The map: **4 of 14 baseline features are conditionally regime-loaded** above the 0.35 ceiling — `btc_ret_14d` 0.4803, `hurst_diff_100_50` 0.4416, `max_dd_window_50` 0.4339, `range_realized_vol_50` 0.3704; `regime_momentum_signed_5d` clean at 0.2092. This is the screening tool for any NEW feature proposed in /078-/080 — a near-zero MARGINAL correlation is necessary but NOT sufficient; the CONDITIONAL (model-split-allocation) correlation is the binding test.

- **`iter-v3/078_axis_guidance`** (Critic Rec #1; reframing finding) — cycle-2 #8 axis is QR-EDA-driven. Per the reframing finding, /078 should target **bull-month entry discrimination** — the IS drag is the bull months where the strategy under-trades the tape. Any NEW feature must be screened against the /077 `conditional_orthogonality.csv` map. **/078-/080 RE-ANCHOR against /077's current-code /060-config baseline (IS +0.8236 / OOS +0.2078)** — NOT the frozen /060 +0.8325/+0.1403 — with the -0.0089/+0.0675 decomposition annotated; the /078 brief Section 0 must state this explicitly. The Kaufman path-efficiency axis and the regime-conditional kill switch (primitive 9) are both CLOSED across two data points — do not re-propose.

## Caveats — iter-v3/078 EXPLORATION-SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT flags

iter-v3/078 (cycle 2 #8 of 10) is a **single-axis UNIVERSE REVISION** — `V3_MODELS` replaces `LDOUSDT` with `ADAUSDT`, the v3 universe going BCH/LDO/TRX → BCH/ADA/TRX. The 14-feature stack, ATR labeling, the 7-primitive risk-gate stack, `ENSEMBLE_SEEDS`, and the Optuna search are all UNCHANGED. The QR EDA-exhausted a NEW-feature candidate (T3/T4) and a meta-labeling M2 candidate (T5) before selecting the swap; EDA T6 is the escapability bound (LDO is the in-universe symbol whose drag is not regime-split-correlated), T7 the IS-edge screen (selects ADA), T8 the holding-time predictor. Verdict SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT per brief Section 8.4; Critic FINAL `75f4d42` OVERALL=MERGE certifies the classification clean (NOT an advancement — SUSPICIOUS axes never advance). Anchor = the re-anchored current-code /060-config baseline IS +0.8236 / OOS +0.2078.

- **`verdict = SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT`** — the ratio gate does NOT fire (OOS/IS monthly Sharpe ratio = +0.6892 / +0.8201 = **0.8404** < 3.0, a healthy ratio) but the **OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode FIRES**: IS shift -0.0035 < 0 (TRUE) AND OOS shift +0.4814 ≥ +0.20 (TRUE). The sub-mode is a separate, independent SUSPICIOUS ground with NO magnitude qualifier. Per the Section 8 disjunctive order (SUSPICIOUS → NULL-RESULT → NEGATIVE → PROMISING → INERT), SUSPICIOUS takes precedence. PROMISING (8.1) fails independently — it requires IS shift ≥ +0.10, observed -0.0035; the eye-catching OOS +0.4814 cannot rescue it. NULL-RESULT is mechanically impossible for a universe swap (74/18 ADA trades absent from /060). NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate; **LDOUSDT is RETAINED**; the universe-revision axis is CLOSED for cycle 2.

- **`hypothesis_falsified — ADA did not lift the IS aggregate`** — brief Section 4.1 predicted IS Δ +0.13 to +0.33 (central +0.20); Section 4.2 falsifier "IS Δ < +0.10 → falsified." Observed IS Δ -0.0035 — the falsifier FIRED. Mechanism: ADA DID trade actively (74 IS trades vs LDO's 11, within the predicted 50-90 range) and ADA's IS contribution IS positive (+21.85 IS weighted_pnl vs LDO -1.66; ADA lifted IS *total* PnL ~45%, IS monthly mean +1.57→+2.09). BUT the IS *Sharpe* stayed flat because **ADA's per-month variance contribution matched its mean contribution** — IS monthly std rose +6.59→+8.83 (+34%) in lockstep with the +33% mean lift; a Sharpe is mean/std, so a co-proportional rise leaves the ratio unchanged. BCH dominates the IS aggregate (~77% of IS weighted_pnl) and absorbed ADA's contribution as added variance — ADA's 74-trade participation entered the aggregate as a denominator driver, not only a numerator driver. The T7 IS-edge screen (+0.617 ADA vs -0.550 LDO, +1.17 margin) measures the per-symbol model in isolation; it correctly predicted ADA's positive *symbol-level* IS edge but did not — and structurally could not — predict the portfolio-aggregate variance impact.

- **`suspicious_doubly_corroborated — the +2.23-candle holding-time falsifier`** (the key forensic finding) — brief Section 4.4 pre-registered an added(ADA)-vs-removed(LDO) roster-composition mean-duration sub-channel per `feedback_v3_is_oos_regime_divergence.md` + Critic /076 Rec #2; T8 predicted the gap at **+0.38 candles** (falsifier > +1.0). The ACTUAL production gap is **+2.226 candles** (ADA IS mean duration ~7.135 vs LDO IS mean 4.909) — 6× the T8 prediction; the Section 4.4 sub-channel falsifier **FIRES**. The Critic recomputed it independently from the trade CSVs: 5 ADAUSDT IS rows resolve `exit_reason = timeout` at exactly 21.0 candles each; LDO had **ZERO** timeout trades (LDO IS durations {20,4,2,1,1,2,7,3,2,4,8} → mean 4.909); even excluding the 5 timeouts the non-timeout gap (6.13−4.91 = +1.22) clears +1.0. Per `feedback_v3_is_oos_regime_divergence.md`, longer-held trades win in the OOS uptrend and bleed in IS bear/chop — so /078's OOS +0.48 lift is the **holding-time-extension regime-divergence mechanism (the /076 trade-selection sub-channel c) arriving via the universe swap**: the swap replaced an entire symbol's model with another symbol's model whose roster is duration-loaded. SUSPICIOUS is DOUBLY corroborated — the OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode fires AND the holding-time channel falsifier fires; the OOS lift is confirmed uncorroborated regime-luck. Reinforced by the per-symbol OOS attribution: ADA's OWN OOS is weak (18 trades, 27.8% WR, net_pnl_pct -14.56%, weighted_pnl +0.4230) — the aggregate OOS lift is dominated by the elimination of LDO's -18.41 OOS weighted_pnl drag + ADA's longer-held trades' regime-luck, NOT by ADA edge (BCH +1.9078 and TRX +24.7184 are bit-frozen).

- **`T6_escapability_premise_incomplete — recorded diagnostic lesson, not a methodology failure`** — EDA T6 was the load-bearing axis justification: it argued LDOUSDT is the one in-universe symbol whose drag is NOT regime-split-correlated (LDO bull-SHORT weak in BOTH windows: IS -1.16 AND OOS -12.58), so removing LDO lifts IS without the IS-up/OOS-down tension. /078 falsified the *conclusion*. T6's reasoning about LDO was NOT wrong — removing LDO did not re-trigger the tension *through LDO*. The defect: **T6 reasoned exclusively about the REMOVED symbol; the regime-loading came from the ADDED symbol** — ADA's longer-held roster (the +2.23-candle channel, the 5 ADA IS timeout trades). T8 was the QR's attempt to bound the added-symbol channel, but T8's label-implied first-touch simulation used screen-grade FIXED barrier parameters and missed the production +2.23 by 6× — the production walk-forward uses Optuna-tuned timeout parameters that produced 5 ADA timeout trades the fixed-parameter screen could not foresee. The QR's brief Section 7 honestly caveated exactly this: *"T6 is evidence that the removal is regime-clean; it is not a proof about the added symbol."* The /076 Critic had flagged the same gap. It is a RECORDED diagnostic lesson — not a methodology failure (Critic Adjudication #3). Corrective (Critic Rec #1): a future universe-revision added-symbol holding-time falsifier must be computed under the production Optuna-tuned parameterization (or with an explicit duration-distribution band wide enough to admit the timeout-trade tail) — a screen-grade fixed-parameter proxy is structurally biased low.

- **`portfolio_aggregate_screen_lesson`** (Critic Rec #2) — an IS-edge screen measures per-symbol Sharpe; it does NOT predict portfolio-aggregate Sharpe lift under BCH dominance. T7 correctly screened ADA at +0.617 per-symbol IS Sharpe, but the production 3-seed walk-forward aggregate is dominated by BCH (~77% of IS weighted_pnl); ADA's added IS variance (+34%) matched its added mean (+33%), pinning the aggregate IS Sharpe flat. Future swap evidence must include a portfolio-aggregate IS-Sharpe sensitivity projection conditioned on the incumbent symbol's IS share — not only the candidate's standalone IS edge. This is the BCH-IS-concentration fragility flag from the /059 baseline, now empirically confirmed for a universe swap.

- **`section_7_calibration — sound; the ≈43% SUSPICIOUS floor was respected and FIRED`** — the brief Section 7 floored the SUSPICIOUS probability at the running cycle-2 base rate of ≈43% (3 of 7 EXPLORATIONs SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT) per Critic /076 Rec #3. SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT fired — the pre-registered weight was vindicated. The QR did NOT float SUSPICIOUS below the base rate (a universe swap is not a bit-identical roster, so the /077 PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC sub-base-rate justification does not apply; and the QR had no conditional-orthogonality *proof* about the *added* symbol). The QR's *central* estimate leaned INERT/PROMISING and was wrong, but the pre-registered failure-mode weight was correctly floored, the predicted sub-mode signature ("IS Δ < 0, OOS Δ ≥ +0.20") matched the observed outcome exactly, and the honest "not a proof about the added symbol" caveat made the verdict a recorded diagnostic lesson. This is the opposite of the /076 calibration miss. The cycle-2 SUSPICIOUS base rate is now **4 of 8** (50%).

- **`bit_stability_verified — no wiring defect`** — the brief Section 4.3 falsifier (BCH/TRX OOS sub-roster bit-stability) does NOT fire. The /077 anchor `comparison.csv` reports OOS BCH +1.9078/37, TRX +24.7184/54; the /078 `comparison.csv` reports BCH +1.9078/37, TRX +24.7184/54 — bit-identical to 4 decimals. LDO -18.4079/12 was replaced by ADA +0.4230/18. The universe swap of the third symbol did not leak into the independent BCH/TRX per-symbol models — single-axis discipline verified, no Phase-6 wiring defect.

- **`iter-v3/079_axis_guidance`** (Critic Rec #3) — cycle-2 #9 axis is QR-EDA-driven. /078 is the **4th cycle-2 SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT** data point (after /071, /073, /076); cycle 2 has now EXHAUSTED feature (T3), meta-labeling M2 (T5), AND universe-revision axes against the LDO structural-weakness problem. Per `feedback_v3_structural_over_knob_exploration.md`, /079 should favor a structural axis (NEW model arch > NEW labeling > NEW risk primitive) and MUST avoid all three exhausted categories. The highest-priority candidate is a NEW model-architecture axis targeting the BCH IS-concentration fragility itself (per-symbol-variance-normalized portfolio-Sharpe aggregation, or inverse-volatility cross-symbol position sizing) — /078 empirically confirmed a positive-edge change to any non-BCH symbol washes against BCH's ~77% IS-PnL dominance. `V3_MODELS` reverts to BCH/LDO/TRX at /079's setup (LDO retained). The Kaufman path-efficiency axis, the regime-conditional kill switch (primitive 9), and the LDO→ADA universe swap are all CLOSED — do not re-propose.

DSR (legacy) = 0.0, PSR = 1.0, DSR_relative_B4 = 0.9999 — Check-3 edge axes INFORMATIONAL at EXPLORATION per `feedback_v3_dsr_mode_artifact.md` (EXPLORATION-mode `n_trials=315` is not comparable to CONFIRMATION-mode `n_trials=1050`). PBO = 0.1277 PASS (near-bit-identical to /077's 0.1278 — PBO is cell-level near-invariant and only the ADA-vs-LDO cells changed). `frac_positive_paths = 0.733` PASS (above /077's 0.6444; CPCV Q25/Q50/Q75 path-Sharpe -0.0268/0.7666/1.4011).

## Caveats — iter-v3/079 EXPLORATION-NULL-RESULT (behavioral saturation) flags

iter-v3/079 (cycle 2 #9 of 10) is a **single-axis NEW RISK PRIMITIVE — primitive 13, a conviction-weighted per-trade position-sizing primitive.** A `conviction_derate(confidence)` map (a-priori constants `C_FLOOR = 0.50`, `C_REF = 0.65`, `W_MIN_FRAC = 0.50`; returns an int weight in `[50, 100]`) is added to `lgbm.py` and wired into `get_signal`, replacing the hardcoded `weight = 100`. It consumes the M1 model's per-trade directional `confidence` (previously computed inside `get_signal` then discarded) and de-rates low-conviction trades' position weight — a pure post-model arithmetic transform; no feature added, no labeling change, no selection-gate change, no Optuna change. The secondary edit reverts /078's closed universe-revision axis: `V3_MODELS` BCH/ADA/TRX → BCH/LDO/TRX (a baseline-restore, not a second axis). Verdict NULL-RESULT (behavioral saturation) per the LOCKED brief Section 8.5; Critic FINAL `20ca0a4` OVERALL=MERGE certifies the classification clean (NOT an advancement — NULL-RESULT axes never advance). Anchor = the re-anchored current-code /060-config baseline IS +0.8236 / OOS +0.2078.

- **`verdict = NULL-RESULT (behavioral saturation)`** — observed IS +0.8288 (Δ +0.0052), OOS +0.2791 (Δ +0.0713), de-rate incidence **7.5% (12/159 IS trades: BCH 6, TRX 6, LDO 0)**. The LOCKED brief Section 8.5 pre-registers NULL-RESULT as the `< 15%`-of-IS-trades-de-rated behavioral-saturation condition, with the explicit note that for a weight-scalar axis the `(symbol,open_time)` key roster is bit-identical by construction so the discriminator is the **de-rated count**, not roster bit-identity. 7.5% < 15% — FIRES. NULL-RESULT is the canonical match on THREE independent grounds: (i) Section 8.5 precedes 8.3 INERT in the disjunctive order; (ii) the `<15%` condition is met exactly; (iii) INERT's own clause requires `≥15%` de-rated, so INERT cannot fire at 7.5%. SUSPICIOUS does NOT fire — the OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode requires `IS shift < 0`, observed IS shift +0.0052 ≥ 0 FAILS that clause (and OOS shift +0.0713 < +0.20 fails the second clause independently); the ratio gate 0.3368 < 3.0. PROMISING fails both gates (IS shift +0.0052 < +0.10, OOS shift +0.0713 < +0.20). NEGATIVE — neither floor breached. NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate.

- **`QE_correctly_overrode_the_dispatch's_suggested_INERT`** (Critic Adjudication #1) — the Phase-6 task dispatch *suggested* an "INERT-AT-EXPLORATION" classification. The QE OVERRODE it and applied the LOCKED brief Section 8 — and the Critic explicitly certified that as CORRECT discipline. The LOCKED Section 8 taxonomy is the pre-registered contract, fixed before the backtest; an orchestrator's dispatch suggestion is not the authority. The QE correctly identified the three independent grounds (INERT requires ≥15% de-rated; 7.5% < 15%; 8.5 precedes 8.3) — a QE that rubber-stamped "INERT" would have misclassified on every one of them. This is the LOCKED-taxonomy-is-the-authority discipline working as designed.

- **`behavioral_saturation — 7.5% vs predicted ≥25%, a 3× behavioral-effect-predictor miss`** (the central finding) — the brief Section 4 predicted ≥25% of IS trades de-rated; the conviction-derate engaged only 7.5%. ROOT CAUSE — a constant-placement defect: the de-rate window is `[C_FLOOR=0.50, C_REF=0.65)`, but the `/067 _inference_threshold_floor = 0.60` is a hard floor — every surviving trade has `confidence ≥ 0.60` — so the *effective* window is the narrow `[0.60, 0.65)` band. The walk-forward Optuna search at n_trials=35 tunes a per-`(symbol,month)` confidence threshold, and those tuned thresholds concentrate **at/above 0.65** — at/above C_REF — collapsing the effective de-rate window toward empty. **C_REF=0.65 was placed at the threshold-saturation point of the confidence distribution, not its busy region.** C_REF was an a-priori guess because the per-trade confidence distribution is not persisted in any report artifact (per-trade `confidence` is computed inside `get_signal` and discarded). Per `feedback_v3_axis_saturation_predictor.md`, a behavioral-effect predictor for an axis gated on a model-internal threshold MUST model the interaction with the Optuna-tuned threshold distribution — the /079 predictor did not.

- **`under-tested_not_dead — the axis is PARKED, not closed`** (Critic Adjudication #3) — the conviction-derate axis is UNDER-TESTED at these a-priori constants, NOT a genuinely dead signal. The directional hypothesis has WEAK BUT REAL support: the 12 de-rated (low-conviction) IS trades DID underperform — mean `net_pnl_pct` **−0.019%** / WR **33.3%** vs the 147 non-de-rated at **+0.476%** / **38.0%**. A genuinely dead axis would show de-rated trades performing AT or ABOVE the non-de-rated group — the opposite of what was observed. Low conviction did mean lower realized edge; the primitive just had almost nothing to act on (7.5% incidence). STRUCTURAL CAVEAT: if Optuna's IS-optimized thresholds intrinsically sit ≥0.65 for a hard 8h directional-classification problem, the surviving signals ARE nearly all high-conviction by construction and a conviction-derate has structurally little to act on regardless of constant choice. CONSEQUENCE — the axis is PARKED: a re-attempt is permissible ONLY with (a) re-designed constants AND (b) the per-trade M1 `confidence` distribution persisted across the walk-forward FIRST, so a re-attempt's C_REF is placed empirically in the populated region. The conviction-derate (primitive 13) is NOT on the closed list (Kaufman path-efficiency, regime-conditional kill switch primitive 9, LDO→ADA universe swap) — it is parked pending the instrumentation.

- **`pure_weight_scalar_structurally_guaranteed — no wiring defect, OOS lift is data-extent`** (Critic Adjudication #2) — the conviction-derate is the LAST statement before `return Signal(...)` and modifies ONLY the `weight` field; every trade-suppressing `NO_SIGNAL` return path executes BEFORE it, unchanged; `conviction_derate` returns an int in `[50,100]` and `test_conviction_derate.py::test_weight_never_zero` verifies across a 10001-point grid it never returns 0 (0 is the only `Signal.weight` that could suppress a trade). Therefore the set of bars emitting `Signal` vs `NO_SIGNAL` CANNOT change — the IS+OOS `(symbol,open_time)` roster is bit-identical to /077's /060-config roster (**0 added, 0 removed**, all six symbol-split pairs verified), IS mean-duration delta **0.0000 candles** (a weight scalar touches no barrier). Because the roster is bit-identical, the OOS +0.0713 Sharpe lift is NOT the conviction-derate producing edge — it is largely a data-extent artifact: the last LDO `end_of_data` trade had its `close_time` extend ~2 candles (~16h) between the /077 and /079 runs (days apart; OOS data extent grew), OOS mean-duration delta +0.0194.

- **`look_ahead_clean — full 5-step chain audit`** (Critic Check 1 / Adjudication #2) — the Critic traced the conviction-derate's data dependency end-to-end: (1) `conviction_derate` is pure arithmetic, no closures/state/IO (`test_conviction_derate.py::TestLookAheadClean` verifies `__closure__ is None` + scans for forbidden refs); (2-3) `confidence` derives from `predict_proba` on test-month bar-close features knowable at the decision point; (4) the models are trained strictly on the embargoed past walk-forward window; (5) the conviction-derate reads `confidence` only after it is already in scope — ZERO new data dependency. A pure arithmetic transform of an already-look-ahead-clean value.

- **`walk_forward_lookahead_bug_is_FIXED — stale citation corrected`** (Critic Rec #3) — the /079 engineering report (and earlier briefs) repeated `feedback_v3_walkforward_lookahead_bug.md`'s claim that the v3 worktree "STILL HAS BUG". The Critic verified `walk_forward.py:113` carries the embargo fix `train_end_ms = test_start_ms − embargo_ms` — **the lookahead bug is FIXED in this worktree.** The fix landed at the iter-v3/058 RE-ANCHOR era (commit `e149e9d`); multiple Critic reviews (/074-/079) have confirmed it. The memory topic file `feedback_v3_walkforward_lookahead_bug.md` and its MEMORY.md index line were CORRECTED at the /079 closeout: historical context retained (the bug existed; all PRE-/058 v3 iterations carried the upward Sharpe bias), but the "still has bug / user paused at /057 to investigate" claim replaced with "FIXED at the iter-v3/058 RE-ANCHOR (`e149e9d`)". /058-onward — the entire /059 canonical baseline and cycles 1-2 post-RE-ANCHOR — run on the FIXED walk-forward; their absolute Sharpe magnitudes are not subject to the bias.

- **`iter-v3/080_axis_guidance`** (Critic Recs #1/#2) — cycle-2 #10 (the LAST cycle-2 EXPLORATION) axis is QR-EDA-driven. Cycle 2 is 0/9 clean PROMISING; the risk-primitive axis is now 2 INERT + 1 NULL-RESULT. HIGHEST-priority /080 candidate: a **PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC that builds the persisted-confidence instrumentation** — add a `confidence` column to `trades.csv` (the ~one-line Phase-6 change) and emit a per-trade M1-confidence distribution artifact (per-symbol, per-IS-month, with the realized Optuna threshold overlaid). This is the precise instrument the /079 axis lacked; as a PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC it is expected to be a bit-identical-roster INERT/NULL-RESULT (no behavioral change), like /077 — its value is the accretive cycle-3 infrastructure, and it discharges the standing /079 Critic Rec #1. SECOND candidate: a NEW model-architecture axis targeting the BCH IS-concentration fragility (per-symbol-variance-normalized portfolio-Sharpe aggregation, or inverse-volatility cross-symbol position sizing) — the /078 Critic Rec #2 priority, still unaddressed. The conviction-derate (primitive 13) is PARKED — do NOT re-attempt it at /080 (it needs the instrumentation first). `V3_MODELS` stays BCH/LDO/TRX (LDO retained). The Kaufman path-efficiency axis, the regime-conditional kill switch (primitive 9), and the LDO→ADA universe swap are all CLOSED — do not re-propose.

DSR (legacy) = 0.0, PSR = 1.0, DSR_relative_B4 = 0.0512 — Check-3 edge axes INFORMATIONAL at EXPLORATION per `feedback_v3_dsr_mode_artifact.md` (EXPLORATION-mode `n_trials=315` is not comparable to CONFIRMATION-mode `n_trials=1050`). PBO = 0.1278 PASS (BIT-IDENTICAL to the /077/060 anchor — a 7.5%-incidence weight scalar leaves the CPCV path distribution unmoved; a clean null signature). `frac_positive_paths = 0.6444` PASS (bit-identical to the /060 anchor).

## Caveats — iter-v3/080 EXPLORATION-NULL-RESULT (bit-identical roster) flags

iter-v3/080 (cycle 2 #10 of 10 — the FINAL cycle-2 EXPLORATION) is a **single-axis PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC** — thread the M1 model's per-trade directional `confidence` scalar (`max(proba_long, proba_short)` from the inner-ensemble mean `predict_proba`, already computed inside `lgbm.py:get_signal`) as pure passive metadata through the `Signal → Order → TradeResult` chain into `trades.csv` (a new `confidence` column), and emit `confidence_distribution.csv` (per-symbol/per-IS-month M1-confidence histograms with the realized per-cell Optuna threshold overlaid). Mandatory secondary edit: REVERT /079's conviction-derate — `lgbm.py:get_signal` back to the flat hardcoded `weight = 100` (a baseline-restore, not a second axis; the `conviction_derate` helper left as harmless unreferenced dead code). No feature / labeling / selection-gate / model-architecture / universe / seed / Optuna change. The brief Section 4 PREDICTED a bit-identical-roster NULL-RESULT (~93%). Verdict NULL-RESULT (bit-identical roster) per the LOCKED brief Section 8.5; Critic FINAL `35fc6de` OVERALL=MERGE certifies the classification clean, the fix-and-rerun methodologically clean, and the persisted-`confidence` deliverable sound (NOT an advancement — a PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC produces no edge ingredient; NULL-RESULT axes never advance). Anchor = the re-anchored current-code /060-config baseline IS +0.8236 / OOS +0.2078.

- **`verdict = NULL-RESULT (bit-identical roster)`** — observed IS +0.8236 (Δ **0.0000**), OOS +0.2908 (Δ +0.0830), OOS/IS ratio 0.3531, IS+OOS roster bit-identical to /077 (0 added / 0 removed, both splits). The LOCKED brief Section 8.5 pre-registers NULL-RESULT as the bit-identical-roster condition for a PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC axis: the `confidence` field is pure passive metadata threaded through `Signal → Order → TradeResult` — it suppresses no trade and touches no barrier — and the /079 conviction-derate revert restores the flat `weight = 100`, so the trade roster IS the /060-config roster. The Critic independently diffed `reports-v3/iteration_v3-080/{in_sample,out_of_sample}/trades.csv` against /077: IS 159 trades (0 keys added, 0 removed), OOS 103 trades (0 keys added, 0 removed), every IS+OOS key and `weight_factor` matching line-for-line — FIRES. NULL-RESULT is the canonical first match in disjunctive order. SUSPICIOUS does NOT fire — the OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode requires `IS shift < 0`, observed IS shift is exactly **0.0000** (the roster is bit-identical so the IS Sharpe is identical by construction) FAILS that clause; OOS shift +0.0830 < +0.20 fails the second clause independently; the ratio gate 0.3531 < 3.0. PROMISING fails both gates (IS shift 0.0000 < +0.10, OOS shift +0.0830 < +0.20). NEGATIVE — neither floor breached. INERT is blocked by 8.5's precedence in the disjunctive order (and the bit-identical roster makes NULL-RESULT, not INERT, the precise pre-registered match: /077 was INERT because 13 TRX IS `weight_factor` values had drifted making its roster *not* byte-identical — /080's roster IS bit-identical, so 8.5 fires exactly). NOT a CONFIRMATION-bundle candidate.

- **`the_fix_and_rerun — empty-confidence-column defect, fix d71adf6, CLEAN AND LEGITIMATE`** (Critic Adjudication #2) — the LOAD-BEARING process event. The first /080 run produced the correct bit-identical-roster NULL-RESULT but the `confidence` column in `trades.csv` was **EMPTY.** ROOT CAUSE: `RiskV2Wrapper.get_signal` (`risk_v2.py`) AND `RiskV3Wrapper.get_signal` (`risk_v3.py`) each *reconstruct* a fresh `Signal` (to apply their position-size scalars) rather than passing the M1 `Signal` through, and the first implementation (`8e7e62b`) missed forwarding `confidence` at BOTH reconstruction sites — so `confidence` was dropped to `None` before reaching `trades.csv`. The QE's OWN engineering report caught the empty column (the engineering-report verification discipline working as designed); the orchestrator authorized a fix-and-rerun. The fix `d71adf6` adds `confidence=sig.confidence` at the exactly-two wrapper Signal-reconstruction sites + an extended wrapper-chain regression test (`tests/strategies/ml/test_confidence_passthrough.py` — routes a `Signal` with a known `confidence` through both wrappers incl. a sub-test forcing the primitive-12 reconstruction branch, plus `TestNoDecisionPathReadsConfidence` static-scanning `src/crypto_trade/*.py` asserting no `if/while .confidence` decision branch so the defect cannot recur). The Critic certified the fix-and-rerun clean on three grounds: (a) the fix is pure passive metadata — at each wrapper reconstruction site the new `Signal`'s `weight` is `sig.weight × scale` arithmetic that does NOT consult `confidence`, and `confidence` is in no gate (`_zscore_ood`, `_hurst_ood`, `_adx_gate_fails`, `_low_vol_filter_fails`, `_per_symbol_cap_scale`, `_vol_scale`, the drawdown brake) and no conditional; (b) the re-run `comparison.csv` is BIT-IDENTICAL to the pre-fix run (IS +0.8236, OOS +0.2908, 159/103) — forwarding a passive metadata field changed no trade/barrier/weight, the classification is invariant; (c) it is a legitimate pre-Critic Phase-6 correction of a *deliverable wiring* defect caught by the QE's own engineering report — NOT a post-BLOCK rerun.

- **`deliverable_verified_sound — the persisted-confidence instrument`** (Critic Adjudication #3) — the /080 deliverable is verified sound. The `confidence` column on `trades.csv` is populated on all **262 IS+OOS trades** with real varied per-trade M1 directional confidence floats — IS `[0.5429, 0.9943]` mean **0.777**; OOS `[0.6257, 0.9981]` mean **0.810**; correctly populated even on `weight_factor = 0.0000` BTC-killed rows. `confidence_distribution.csv` is non-degenerate — 2016 data rows, 315 non-zero histogram bins, genuine per-symbol/per-IS-month histograms over the a-priori `conf_bin_edges` grid with the realized per-symbol last-month Optuna threshold overlaid per cell. WHAT THE DISTRIBUTION REVEALS: it empirically confirms the /079 NULL-RESULT root cause — the realized per-symbol Optuna confidence thresholds cluster at **0.5859 / 0.6507 / 0.6893** and the surviving trades sit at/above them, so the /079 conviction-derate's `[0.50, 0.65)` de-rate window was nearly empty. /080 DISCHARGES the /079 Critic Rec #1 methodology debt — a future cycle-3 conviction-derate re-attempt can now place `C_REF` empirically in the populated confidence region rather than guessing.

- **`look_ahead_clean — the confidence threading + the OOS +0.083 data-extent artifact`** (Critic Check 1 / Adjudication #3) — `confidence` is sourced at `lgbm.py:672-673` (`directional_conf = max(float(proba[0]), float(proba[2]))`) from `proba` = the inner-ensemble mean of `predict_proba` — the IDENTICAL `proba` vector already driving `direction` (line 723) and the existing `_confidence_threshold` selection gate (line 678). Threading it into the `Signal` introduces ZERO new data dependency (`proba` is already in scope and already past-only — the models are trained strictly on the embargoed past walk-forward window; the /079 Critic verified `proba` past-only). The fix `d71adf6` only forwards this already-clean value. `_write_confidence_distribution()` is pure post-backtest report emission (no model retraining, no OOS quantity consulted, no decision path). Foundation Audit confirms `walk_forward.py:113` carries the embargo fix `train_end_ms = test_start_ms − embargo_ms` — the lookahead bug is FIXED in this worktree. THE OOS +0.0830 IS A SINGLE-TRADE DATA-EXTENT ARTIFACT, NOT AN AXIS EFFECT: because the roster is bit-identical, a CSV column cannot produce edge — the Critic isolated the single OOS divergence `(LDOUSDT, 1778716799999)`: a bit-identical key with bit-identical `weight_factor = 0.7700`, `close_time` extended +16h, `pnl_pct` 1.8051 → 6.3435 — exactly one `end_of_data` trade resolving with 2 more candles because the OOS data extent grew between the /077 and /080 runs. This is the IDENTICAL data-extent mechanism the /077 (+0.0675) and /079 (+0.0713) Critics certified benign.

- **`hypothesis_calibration — brief predicted NULL-RESULT ~93%; it fired; calibration sound`** — the brief Section 4 PREDICTED a bit-identical-roster NULL-RESULT with ~93% confidence; the brief Section 7 pre-registered a low SUSPICIOUS probability (well below the running cycle-2 base rate), legitimately justified by the structural argument that a CSV-only PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC is structurally holding-time-orthogonal and roster-orthogonal (the same proof-strong-enough-to-deviate-below-the-base-rate ground the /077 PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTIC used). The outcome — NULL-RESULT (bit-identical roster), IS shift exactly 0.0000 — is exactly what was predicted; the ~93% prediction fired. The one nuance: the realized residual ~7% risk turned out to be a *deliverable-quality* risk (the empty-`confidence`-column wiring defect), NOT a *classification* risk — the classification was never in doubt (the roster could not move); the risk that materialized was whether the deliverable would be delivered, resolved by the fix-and-rerun. The cross-cutting lesson: a deliverable-instrumentation axis must verify the instrument is non-degenerate before the classification is read — the QE engineering-report discipline caught it. Calibration sound on both the taxonomy (Section 8.5) and the probability (low SUSPICIOUS, high NULL-RESULT). Cycle-2 SUSPICIOUS base rate unchanged at **4 of 10** (/071, /073, /076, /078).

- **`iter-v3/081_axis_guidance`** (Critic Recs #1/#2/#3) — iter-v3/080 closes the cycle-2 EXPLORATION sequence: **cycle 2 EXPLORATIONs are 10/10 COMPLETE** with **0 clean PROMISING** (4 SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT /071/073/076/078, 1 NEGATIVE /072, 3 INERT /074/075/077, 2 NULL-RESULT /079/080 — where /077 + /080 were the two PASSIVE-DIAGNOSTICs). The NEXT iteration is the **iter-v3/081 cycle-2 CONFIRMATION** — and on current evidence there is no edge ingredient to bundle, so /081 is a **/059-baseline multi-seed re-validation** (a `--seeds 2` run of the canonical /059 configuration to confirm BASELINE_V3.md's IS +1.0894 / OOS +0.5791 still reproduces under the current code state), by direct analogy to cycle 1's /070 CONFIRMATION. The /081 brief must pre-register the re-validation branch and carry the three standing unresolved constraints into cycle 3: (a) LDO directional weakness; (b) the BCH ~77% IS-PnL concentration fragility (the /078 finding); (c) the bull-month entry-discrimination drag (the /077 reframing). For any cycle-3 conviction-derate re-attempt: place `C_REF` against the per-trade `confidence` column on `trades.csv` (build a fresh per-symbol/per-month histogram from it), NOT the `confidence_distribution.csv` last-month overlay; and first demonstrate from /080's persisted column that a materially-populated below-threshold de-rate band exists, else the axis re-saturates. The conviction-derate (primitive 13) remains PARKED. CYCLE 3 must be a STEP-CHANGE in ambition per the NEW memory `feedback_v3_bold_research_mandate.md` (user directive 2026-05-16) — genuine WebSearch/WebFetch literature + crypto-native-alpha research (funding rates, open interest, basis, liquidation cascades, on-chain flow), aggressive symbol-universe exploration (v3 has been LOCKED to BCH/LDO/TRX for ALL of cycles 1-2; the allowed universe excludes only v1/v2 symbols + MKR, so dozens of symbols are available), and bold structural axes (NEW crypto-native feature families, NEW model architectures, multi-symbol-pooled models) — NOT the gate-knob / risk-primitive / single-symbol-swap / labeling-tweak / instrumentation families cycle 2 exhausted. ## Cycle-note — the /060 EXPLORATION anchor is STALE; cycle-2 EXPLORATIONs /078+ RE-ANCHOR (established iter-v3/077 closeout per Critic FINAL `46e9fe9` Rec #1)

> Pre-registered at the iter-v3/077 closeout. /077 is the first iteration since /060 to run the exact /060 14-feature config with no axis, and it does NOT reproduce the frozen /060 anchor.

**Finding.** The /060 EXPLORATION-MODE-REFERENCE has been quoted as IS +0.8325 / OOS +0.1403 by every cycle-2 EXPLORATION (/071-/076). That frozen value no longer reproduces on current code + current data. The current-code /060-config baseline — measured by /077, which reverts /076's feature and adds only a report-emission function, so /077's backtest IS the /060 configuration — is **IS +0.8236 / OOS +0.2078**.

**Exact additive decomposition:**

| Component | Δ | Mechanism | Behavior over calendar time |
|---|---:|---|---|
| IS code-drift | **-0.0089** | iter-v3/061 TRX `vol_scale_floor=0.5` floors 13 IS TRX `weight_factor` values (4 wins / 9 losses → net IS wpnl -0.1616) | PERMANENT, deterministic — a fixed offset; any post-/061 /060-config run carries it |
| OOS data-extent | **+0.0675** | the 2026-05 OOS month post-dates /060's data fetch (data-extent A+B = +2.1248 wpnl; TRX-floor C contributes +0.5943) | GROWS monotonically with calendar time |

**Why this is a methodology correction, not cheating.** Re-anchoring replaces a stale reference value with the freshest reproducible no-axis run of the canonical config. It does NOT shift the OOS-cutoff (immutable 2025-03-24), does NOT trim a start date, and does NOT change the measurement window — it corrects the *reference value* against which intra-cycle deltas are reported.

**The /078+ re-anchoring decision (formally adopted).** Cycle-2 EXPLORATIONs **/078, /079, /080 anchor against the current-code /060-config baseline IS +0.8236 / OOS +0.2078** — NOT the frozen +0.8325/+0.1403. The -0.0089 (code-drift) / +0.0675 (data-extent) decomposition is recorded. The /078 brief Section 0 MUST state which anchor it uses and stop treating the frozen +0.1403 OOS as reproducible. Per-iteration data-extent drift from /077 onward is small (iterations run days apart), so /078-/080 deltas will be cleaner than /071-/076's were. The classification noise bands (±0.10 IS / ±0.20 OOS) remain interpretable under the corrected anchor.

**Robustness — no cycle-2 classification flips.** The IS code-drift (-0.0089) is an order of magnitude inside the INERT IS noise band (±0.10); the OOS data-extent drift (+0.0675) is well inside the ±0.20 OOS band. /071/073/076 were classified SUSPICIOUS by the OOS/IS *ratio* gate (4.51 / 6.85 / 15.04) — a ±0.07 OOS shift cannot move a ratio across the 3.0 boundary for those magnitudes. /074/075 were INERT by noise-band shifts that already absorbed the same drift. **The cycle-2 CONFIRMATION (iter-v3/081+) is unaffected** — its MERGE gates anchor on BASELINE_V3.md /059 (canonical, tag `v0.v3-059`); the /060 EXPLORATION-MODE-REFERENCE is an intra-cycle delta anchor only and never feeds the CONFIRMATION gate.

## Cycle-note — the v3 IS drag is in the BULL months, not bear/chop (reframing finding, established iter-v3/077 closeout)

> Pre-registered at the iter-v3/077 closeout per Critic FINAL `46e9fe9` Adjudication #3. EDA T1 re-stratifies /060's IS monthly PnL by an exogenous per-calendar-month BTC-regime label.

**Finding.** /074, /075, and /076 all targeted "the IS bear/chop drag" — but they used the *strategy's own endogenous trade-level regime tag* (a label conditioned on where the strategy chose to trade and how those trades resolved — partially circular when used to stratify performance). EDA T1 re-stratifies /060's IS monthly PnL by an **exogenous** per-calendar-month BTC-regime label (`build_btc_monthly_regime`: a month is BULL if ≥50% of BTC 8h bars have `close[t-1] > SMA_270[t-1]`; `.shift(1)` before the rolling SMA — past-only):

| IS stratum (per-month BTC-regime) | Monthly Sharpe | Months | % positive |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| IS_BEAR_CHOP | **+1.2909** | 15 | 47% |
| IS_BULL | **+0.4100** | 18 | 33% |

**The IS drag is in the BULL months.** The Critic certified the per-month BTC-regime label methodologically superior — it is exogenous to the strategy (computed purely from BTCUSDT OHLCV), where the prior trade-level tag is endogenous. The two labels disagree, and the exogenous one is the correct instrument.

**Consequence — three cycle-2 iterations optimized against the wrong stratum.** /074, /075, and the /076 framing all aimed at bear/chop. The structural IS drag is the bull months, where the strategy under-trades the tape. The EDA T6 escapability synthesis sharpens the diagnosis: win/loss duration ratio 2.08, 64% stop-loss exits, bull-month WR 32.7% vs bear/chop WR 43.6% — the IS problem is a **directional-quality / entry-discrimination** problem in bull regimes, not a holding-time or position-sizing problem in bear/chop. Cycle-2 EXPLORATIONs /078-/080 should target bull-month entry discrimination, screening any NEW feature against the /077 `conditional_orthogonality.csv` map.

## Cycle-level finding — v3 IS/OOS structural regime divergence (pre-registered per iter-v3/073 Critic Rec #2)

> Pre-registered at the iter-v3/074 closeout to discharge the iter-v3/073 Critic FINAL `c05c95e` Recommendation #2 (the catalog-level entry was not previously added). Codified in `feedback_v3_is_oos_regime_divergence.md`.

**Finding.** v3's IS and OOS windows are structurally divergent regimes. The IS window (2022-09 → 2025-03) spans the 2022 bear, 2023 chop, the 2024 bull, and the 2024-Q4/2025-Q1 deceleration — a mixed regime that **penalizes longer-held trades** (in bear/chop, a trade held longer simply bleeds longer). The OOS window (2025-03 → 2026-05) is a persistent BCH/LDO/TRX uptrend that **rewards longer-held trades**. Any axis that EXTENDS effective trade holding time mechanically loads this IS/OOS regime difference: OOS Sharpe soars, IS Sharpe collapses.

**Evidence chain — 3-SUSPICIOUS-then-1-orthogonal-confirmation:**

| Iter | Axis | Holding-time effect | IS Δ | OOS Δ | OOS/IS ratio (within-iter `comparison.csv`) | Classification |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| /065 | Universal SL widening (2.0,1.0)→(2.0,1.5) | EXTENSION (wider SL → trades ride longer) | -0.28 | +0.71 | **1.56** | SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT (sub-mode) |
| /071 | Meta-labeling (same-feature M2 take/skip) | EXTENSION (M2 veto removes early stop-outs) | -0.12 | +0.63 | **0.68** | SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT (sub-mode) |
| /073 | Per-symbol triple-barrier asymmetry (BCH/LDO SL→1.25) | EXTENSION (wider SL arm; LDO shorter TP arm) | -0.55 | +1.76 | **6.85** | SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT (ratio gate) |
| /074 | Regime-conditional kill switch (primitive 9, TRX-scoped) | **ORTHOGONAL** (kept-roster duration delta −0.39 IS / −0.24 OOS — slightly NEGATIVE) | +0.013 | +0.069 | **0.247** | **INERT** — SUSPICIOUS did NOT fire |
| /076 | NEW feature `range_efficiency_50` (Kaufman path efficiency, 15th feature) | **ORTHOGONAL per-trade** (62 common OOS trades duration delta 0.000 — identical barriers) **but loaded via SELECTION** (40 added OOS trades mean 7.25 candles vs 40 removed at 6.03; full-roster OOS Δ +0.480, below the +1.0 falsifier) | -0.79 | +0.51 | **15.04** | SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT (ratio gate + OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode) |
| /078 | UNIVERSE REVISION (replace LDOUSDT with ADAUSDT) | **loaded via the ADDED symbol's roster** — the swap replaces a whole symbol's model; ADA's roster is +2.226 candles longer-held than LDO's was (ADA IS mean ~7.135 vs LDO IS mean 4.909), driven by 5 ADA IS timeout trades at 21.0 candles vs LDO's ZERO timeouts; added-vs-removed gap +2.23 ≫ the +1.0 falsifier (T8 predicted +0.38 — missed 6× by a screen-grade fixed-parameter proxy) | -0.0035 | +0.4814 | **0.8404** | SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT (OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode only) |

(Ratios are the CORRECTED within-iteration `comparison.csv` `monthly_sharpe` values — the /074 engineering report's "5.04/4.51/12.55" and the /073 diary's "2.87/4.51/6.85" are superseded. /073 and /076 trip the >3.0 ratio gate; /065, /071 and /078 fired SUSPICIOUS on the OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode; /076 fires on BOTH grounds. /078's ratio is a healthy 0.84 — the sub-mode is the firing trigger, NOT the ratio gate; the ratio is healthy because the IS shift is flat -0.0035, not the large IS collapse of /071/073/076.)

**Confirmation.** /074 was deliberately a holding-time-ORTHOGONAL axis. It did NOT reproduce the SUSPICIOUS pattern — OOS/IS ratio 0.247, and the kept-roster duration delta is slightly NEGATIVE (opposite direction from the extension family). Three holding-time-extension axes loaded the regime factor; one holding-time-orthogonal axis did not. The diagnosis is structural to v3's data split, not a property of any one axis.

**MATERIAL EXTENSION (iter-v3/076 — the regime factor can be loaded via trade SELECTION, not only holding-time EXTENSION).** The /065/071/073 mechanism was holding-time EXTENSION via barrier mechanics — a wider SL, a meta-label veto removing early stop-outs, a per-symbol barrier rebalanced toward TP all lengthen per-trade duration. /076 demonstrated a SECOND, distinct mechanism. `range_efficiency_50` has NO barrier-extension mechanism whatsoever: it does not touch the SL, the TP, the timeout, or the labeling — it is a model feature added to a frozen labeling stack. Its 62 common OOS trades have a duration delta of exactly 0.000 (identical barriers, mechanically forced). Yet it still produced the clearest SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT signature in cycle 2 (OOS/IS 15.04). The channel: the feature changed WHICH trades the model SELECTS — it dropped 40 OOS trades (mean 6.03 candles) and added 40 different OOS trades (mean 7.25 candles), skewing the OOS roster toward longer-held trades. In the OOS sustained uptrend, longer-held trades score HIGH on path efficiency; the model learned to prefer high-efficiency moments, and that preference is regime-correlated even though the feature's marginal value distribution is not (EDA T3 |regime-sign corr| 0.010). The regime factor was loaded by **trade-selection composition**, not barrier extension. The discriminating diagnostic is therefore NOT the full-roster mean-duration shift alone (which stayed below the +1.0 falsifier at +0.480) — it is the **added-vs-removed roster-composition mean-duration gap** (7.25 vs 6.03). A feature can pass the holding-time-extension falsifier and still load the regime factor via selection.

**MATERIAL EXTENSION (iter-v3/078 — a UNIVERSE SWAP is a THIRD vector for the regime factor: the ADDED symbol's roster can be duration-loaded even when the REMOVED symbol is regime-clean).** Established iter-v3/078 closeout per Critic FINAL `75f4d42`.

The /065/071/073 mechanism is holding-time EXTENSION via barrier mechanics. The /076 mechanism is trade SELECTION via a NEW feature on a frozen labeling stack. /078 demonstrated a THIRD, distinct vector. iter-v3/078 changed neither the barriers nor the feature set — it replaced one symbol (`LDOUSDT`) with another (`ADAUSDT`) in `V3_MODELS`, swapping out a whole symbol's per-symbol model for another's. The EDA T6 escapability bound argued LDO is the in-universe symbol whose drag is NOT regime-split-correlated — and T6 was correct *about the removed symbol*: removing LDO did not re-trigger the tension through LDO. But the regime factor was loaded by the **ADDED symbol**: ADA's production roster is **+2.226 candles longer-held** than LDO's was (ADA IS mean duration ~7.135 vs LDO IS mean 4.909), driven by **5 ADA IS timeout trades at 21.0 candles** where LDO had **ZERO** timeout trades. The brief Section 4.4 pre-registered an added-vs-removed mean-duration sub-channel falsifier (threshold +1.0 candle) and T8 predicted the gap at +0.38 candles — but T8 used screen-grade FIXED barrier parameters and missed the production +2.23 by 6×, because the production walk-forward uses Optuna-tuned timeout parameters that produced timeout trades the fixed-parameter screen could not foresee. In v3's OOS sustained uptrend, longer-held trades win; ADA's duration-loaded roster therefore lifted OOS (+0.48) while the IS stayed flat — SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT, doubly corroborated (the OOS-DOMINANT sub-mode AND the +2.23-candle holding-time falsifier both fire). ADA's OWN OOS edge is weak (18 trades, 27.8% WR, weighted_pnl +0.4230) — the OOS lift is the elimination of LDO's -18.41 OOS drag + ADA's longer-held trades' regime-luck, not ADA edge.

**The discriminating diagnostic for a universe swap** is therefore the **added(swap-in)-vs-removed(swap-out) roster-composition mean-duration gap, computed under the PRODUCTION Optuna-tuned barrier parameterization** — a screen-grade fixed-parameter proxy is structurally biased low (it under-predicts the timeout-trade tail). T6-style escapability evidence about the *removed* symbol is necessary but NOT sufficient: a universe swap can load the regime factor through the *added* symbol's roster even when the removed symbol is provably regime-clean.

**Consequence for the cycle agenda.** The holding-time-extension axis family is SATURATED — it will reproduce SUSPICIOUS-OOS-DOMINANT if re-tried. Per `feedback_v3_is_oos_regime_divergence.md`, every EXPLORATION brief Section 2 must include a holding-time-effect predictor; any axis that lengthens mean/median trade duration loads the regime factor and must be rejected at brief stage. **NEW per /076:** the predictor must ALSO include an added-vs-removed roster-composition mean-duration sub-channel — a feature-axis that shifts the OOS roster toward longer-held trades loads the factor via selection even with no barrier change. **NEW per /078:** for a universe-revision axis, the added-vs-removed sub-channel must compare the SWAP-IN vs SWAP-OUT symbol rosters AND must be computed under the production Optuna-tuned barrier parameters — a screen-grade fixed-parameter proxy under-predicts the timeout-trade tail (T8 missed the production gap 6×). And a NEW "regime-orthogonal" feature must be validated for CONDITIONAL orthogonality (model-split-allocation / SHAP attribution vs the regime label), not just MARGINAL orthogonality (raw feature value vs the regime label) — /076 proved marginal orthogonality is necessary but NOT sufficient. The cycle-2 agenda must use axes that are holding-time-orthogonal, selection-roster-orthogonal, universe-roster-orthogonal, AND (for feature axes) conditionally-orthogonal, to address the IS bear/chop drag (brief /074 EDA PART 1: IS bear/chop monthly Sharpe −0.0242, 27.8% positive months). With /078 closed, cycle 2 has exhausted feature, meta-labeling M2, AND universe-revision axes against the LDO structural-weakness problem — cycle-3 priorities should pivot toward the BCH IS-concentration / portfolio-aggregation architecture problem /078 surfaced.
